# Cost model — v0.3 (design estimate; Graph and Posts/Timeline rows use emulator-measured numbers, no production data yet)
Owner: sre-performance (maintains actuals weekly). Seeded by: architect, 2026-09-26, from ADR-0002…0007 and the protos.

Updated 2026-10-27 (W3-23, sre-performance): ADR-0024 Part A (profile tabs, likes list, pins, banners, "Follows you", who to follow) added to §1-§6, §9 and the new §12 (emulator runs on alternate ports and the repository budget tests; nothing was run against a cloud project). The delta is **additive** on top of the W2 delta and, as for W2, leaves the §2/§3 base (230.0 reads/DAU) and the ADR-0022 inputs of §5 (232.3) untouched: Part A is **+51.5 reads, +0.06 writes, +0.005 deletes, +2.3 requests per DAU per day, +0.30 Vision units per DAU-month and +1.34 MB of banner egress per DAU-month** (the ADR-0024 plan said +40.2 reads; the whole difference is the who-to-follow call mix, §2 notes). Rebased on `main` 16524df: the W3-22 fixes #350 (L-1, L-5) and #351 (M-3, L-4) are folded in where they change a figure (banner Finalize writes, §4 M-3 and L-4, the warm suggestions read). The line goes from 35.7 to **17.2 reads/DAU** (0.63 calls per DAU, 27.6 reads per call, cold cap 32), Part A from 51.5 to **33.0 reads**, the whole product from 300.2 to **281.7 reads/DAU** (free read quota out at about **177 DAU**, was 167; 80% line at about 142, was 133) and requests from 40.0 to 39.6. Who to follow is **6.1%** of all reads against its unchanged 10% line (§6). Stage 0 bill with W2 and Part A: about INR **465 / 1,692 / 3,708** at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU (was 471 / 1,711 / 3,773). Updated 2026-10-28 (W3-37, sre-performance): ADR-0025 Part B (private accounts and follow requests) added to §1-§6, §9 and the new §13 (k6 `loadtest/private_accounts_w3b.js` on emulators on the 34xxx port block, plus the repository's `budgettest` lines; nothing was run against a cloud project). The delta is **additive** on the W2 and Part A figures, priced at the margin: **+1.25 reads per DAU per day on the request path (1.23 with the measured call costs; about 1.51 with the two founder-accepted allowances priced at their upper bounds), +0.16 writes, +0.05 deletes and +0.1 requests, plus a detached protected-set refresh of at most 1 read per instance per 60 s** (960 / 2,400 / 4,320 reads a day at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU; the last is the cap of 3 instances x 1,440 minutes). The refresh runs on a detached counter (`protected_set_reads`) and is **not in `fs_reads`**: every daily total must add it (§9; `make cost` now prints the column). Whole product with W2, Part A and Part B: 282.95 reads, 33.76 writes, 8.45 deletes, 39.7 requests per DAU plus the refresh; free read quota out at about **175 DAU** (was 177), the 80% line (the 40k/day alert) at about **140** (was 142). Part B alone is **about INR 1.4 / 4.4 / 12.7 a month at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU** against the ADR's 1.4 / 4.2 / 10 (the 2.7 at 3,000 DAU is Cloud Run vCPU-s and egress the ADR did not price, the pattern of the W2-15 note); the Stage 0 bill with W2, Part A and Part B is about **INR 466 / 1,696 / 3,721**. Every RPC held its documented budget. Changes are marked `2026-10-28 W3-37`.

Method: `free-tier-budget` §2. Every RPC comment in `proto/` carries the same worst/typical numbers; change both together.

## 1. Assumptions (replace with measured values after the first 100 users)
| Assumption | Value | Why / sensitivity |
|---|---|---|
| Sessions (app opens) per DAU/day | 4 | drives GetMe, refreshes |
| Home refreshes per DAU/day | 8 (pull + resume; auto-refresh ≥ 60 s apart) | each costs `C` = ceil((F+1)/30) reads even if empty |
| Median following F | 60 → C = 3 incl. self; measured 2-4 reads overhead/refresh (graph cold 4, warm 3, all warm 2); modelled 4. `userLikes` is not read until P5 and is not in any row | a user following 300 pays ~11/refresh |
| Posts per DAU/day (incl. replies) | 1.0 (30% replies) | conservative; lurker-heavy apps see 0.2–0.5 |
| New followee posts seen per DAU/day | 60 (each read once via `since`) | biggest single read line; scales with F × activity |
| Older pages (infinite scroll) per DAU/day | 1 × 20 items | |
| Likes / reposts / follows per DAU/day | 5 / 0.3 / 0.5 | likes dominate writes |
| Images per DAU/day | 0.2 (≈ 0.15 upload calls) | drives GCS + Vision |
| Vision units per image (2026-10-23 ADR-0022) | **2**: the full image and its thumbnail are both screened (`VISION_SCREEN_THUMB` default true, set true in prod and dev Terraform; `backend/internal/media/service.go` `finalizeOne`: `units := int64(1); if s.ScreenThumb { units = 2 }`) | 0.2 images x 2 x 30.42 days = **12.17 units per DAU-month** (was 6 at 1 unit per image). Setting `VISION_SCREEN_THUMB=false` halves it |
| Instance cache hit rate | ~50% for users/graph/post docs (assumption; never measured, no traffic yet) | low at Stage 0: few concurrent users, scale-to-zero empties caches |
| Avg billable Cloud Run time per request | 0.1 s at 1 vCPU (low concurrency, little overlap) | conservative: overlap at higher traffic lowers it |
| Avg API response (binary proto, gzip) | 6 KB | |
| Image sizes | thumb ≈ 40 KB, full ≈ 250 KB, avatar thumb ≈ 4 KB; pair stored ≈ 300 KB | |
| Uncached image GETs per DAU/day | ~30 (20 thumbs, 10 avatars) + 3 full views | client disk cache for repeats |
| Web share of DAU | 30% (Hosting transfer counts web API via rewrite + static) | mobile calls `run.app` directly (ADR-0007) |
| Bookmarks per DAU/day (2026-10-26 W2-15, ADR-0023 assumptions) | 0.3 AddBookmark, 0.1 RemoveBookmark, 0.3 first-page opens of `/bookmarks` and 0.1 older pages (page size 20, the client default); posts mostly not in the instance cache; plus an assumed 0.02 no-op or retried Add/Remove calls (5% of 0.4) at 1 write each | the list is 16.9 of the 18.7 reads/DAU W2 adds; the ADR-0023 trigger (15% of daily reads) needs about 2.2x this list usage |
| Quote calls per DAU/day (2026-10-26 W2-15, W2-11 assumption) | 0.05 (5% of the 1.0 posts/DAU, about one sixth of the 0.3 reposts); added on top of the posts line, not carved out of it | W2-11 shows Quote under the existing `engagement` + `quotes` flags (dev allowlist 3); a quote is a root post for feeds, so carving it out of the 1.0 would lower the delta by 0.11 reads, 0.2 writes, immaterial |
| Shared-link web visitors (2026-10-26 W2-15, W2-8/W2-9) | not a per-DAU line: first-time visitors per day against the Hosting headroom, §5 and §11 | a visitor who is not signed in downloads the whole bundle once and makes no API call |
| Profile tabs, likes list, pins, banners (2026-10-27 W3-23, ADR-0024 call mix) | per DAU per day: 0.30 Media-tab and 0.15 Reposts-tab first-page opens, 0.2 first-page opens of History > Likes, 0.6 `GetPost` of a pinned post, 0.03 `UpdateProfile` with extras, 0.005 banner uploads, 2 `GetProfileRelationship` (replacing 2 of the 3 `GetRelationships` calls, net +0.5 read each), 0.05 deleted posts that reach the pin job | tabs and the likes list have no instance result cache, so every open reads its page; the 15% tabs-plus-likes trigger needs about 3.6x this usage |
| Who-to-follow call mix (2026-10-28 Amendment 2; was 2026-10-27 W3-23) | **0.63** `ListSuggestedUsers` per DAU per day: 0.6 with the 48 h client cache (an active account calls unless it fetched the day before; with p = 2/3 of the day's DAU active yesterday, f = 1 / (1 + p); best case 0.5), 0.02 refills (B2 rule 4) and about 0.01 empty-answer refetches; 95% of the calls cold on the server (the 1 h per-viewer entry only helps a second device or a re-login within the hour); of the cold calls 85% friends of friends (**32** reads at `SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` 20, was 42) and 15% curated (12 reads: accounts that follow fewer than 3, or whose graph yields fewer than 3 candidates); 5% warm at 1 to 2 | **Planning 27.6 reads per call** (0.95 x (0.85 x 32 + 0.15 x 12) + 0.05 x 1.5; cold mean 29.0), **17.2 reads/DAU** (0.6 x 27.6 + 0.66 for the refills and empty refetches) and **6.1% of all reads** (17.2 of 281.7), under the 10% trigger of §6. Before: 1.0 call x 35.7 = 11.9% (24 h cache, edge limit 30, cap 42) |
| Private accounts and follow requests (2026-10-28 W3-37, ADR-0025 call mix) | per DAU per day: 5% of DAU protected; **0.05** Follows to a protected target (they are 0.05 of the 0.5 Follows, so the reads stay and a Follow costs 3 writes instead of 5); **0.005** withdraws; **0.02** first-page `ListFollowRequests` at page 20 (protected callers only, so the 5% is already inside the call rate); **0.04** `RespondToFollowRequest` (all counted as accepts: 4 writes); **0.002** privacy toggles; GetMe **4 x 5% = 0.2** calls that pay the request count (cached 30 s: **0.10** reads); **0.05 / 0.04** `follow_request` / `follow_accepted` notifications; the `privacy_public` job, eraser and export per event | the lists are 0.84 of the 1.25 reads; the protected share scales GetMe, the lists and the notifications linearly, so at 20% protected (four times the assumption) the request path is about 4.9 reads/DAU, 1.7% of all reads; the refresh below does not depend on it |
| Protected-set refresh (2026-10-28 W3-37, ADR-0025 D3) | 1 read per instance per `CACHE_TTL` (60 s) while the instance serves a request that consults the set; 0 while idle; singleflight. **Instance-time assumption behind 960 / 2,400 / 4,320 a day:** an instance counts as serving in every minute where it handles one such request, so 300 DAU is 16 instance-hours a day, 1,000 DAU 40, and 3,000 DAU 72 = 3 instances x 24 h, the `max-instances` cap (3 x 1,440 = 4,320). Reads per DAU: 3.2 / 2.4 / 1.4 | a ceiling, not a forecast: it is paid even with 0 protected accounts, never rises with traffic past the cap, and is 8.6% of the free daily reads at most. Two points between the ADR's are interpolated here (100 DAU 320 a day, 600 DAU 1,577), not asserted |
| Messaging call mix (2026-10-29 W7-23, ADR-0027 assumptions, kept) | per DAU per day: **1.5** SendMessage (5% open a new conversation), 1.5 push deliveries (one per message; the Pub/Sub push to `/internal` is a request), 0.5 first-page and 1.5 `since` ListConversations, 0.5 first-page and 1.0 `since`-resume ListMessages, **5 polls** (a visible thread: web hot 15 s, Android hot 30 s with push, 45 s quiet, stopped after 5 min idle or hidden), 1.0 MarkConversationRead, 4 GetMe with the badge `count()` (about 75% miss the 30 s cache), 0.05 accept / delete / report / eraser / export | every rate is an input with no traffic behind it. The sensitivities are messages sent (each extra message per DAU per day costs about 7 reads: 2.5 for the send, 3.5 for the push and 1 for the recipient's read, plus 3.1 writes and 2 requests) and polls (each extra poll per DAU per day is INR 0.43 / 1.4 / 4.3 for reads at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU; only at 3,000 DAU are the request and vCPU quotas already passed, adding 3.5 for the request and up to 20.8 for 100 ms of vCPU, **INR 28.6** in all) |
| Poll behaviour (2026-10-29 W7-23, measured in §14) | the `since` token trails the newest message by the 5 s settle window, so a poll of a quiet thread **never comes back empty**: it returns the newest message again (**1 read warm, 3 cold**, about 250 bytes of binary proto after gzip). A poll inside a conversation that is being written to returns every message of the last 5 s: up to a page (measured 50 reads a poll during a 50 sends/s burst). Measured mean over 4,500 polls in a 300-user herd: **1.41 reads** (1.43 for every poll after a user's first), 87% of polls at 1 read, 13% at 2 | the plan's 1.2 planning value is +17% low; the model uses 1.41. One instance only: with three warm instances a user's profile and conversation entries are cached per instance, so more polls land cold (3 reads); the all-cold ceiling is 15 reads per DAU for 5 polls against the 7.05 used |
| Search, trends and Explore call mix (2026-10-31 W4-23, ADR-0028 / plan assumptions, kept; hit rates are assumptions) | per DAU per day: **1.5** typeahead SearchPeople calls (limit 5; debounce 300 ms, 2 characters; about 0.5 search sessions of 3 calls), 0.3 People-tab calls (limit 20), **1.0** SearchPosts page-1 calls (TOP, LATEST or MEDIA of one tag; 0.5 hashtag taps + 0.5 typed searches), 0.3 older pages or Media scans (a quarter of them Media scans, an assumption), **2.0** GetTrends calls (Explore + side column, 5-minute client cache) **plus 1.5 GetTrends calls from the typeahead** (the client asks for `#tag` and word suggestions on each debounced query) = **6.6 requests**; tag-entry hit rate **40%** and people-prefix hit rate 50% (both **assumptions, never measured**; the 60 s per-instance entries and 3 instances make them optimistic for typed searches); 1.0 posts per DAU per day for the trend refresh | the hit rate of the tag entry is the one input that moves the W4 line: page 1 costs 33 - 30h reads, W4 is 50.9 / 38.9 / 29.9 reads per DAU at h = 0 / 40% / 70% (§15); `cache_hit` on the request line measures it |
| W6 call mix (2026-10-31 W6-21, ADR-0029 / plan assumptions, kept; reads re-measured in §16) | per DAU per day: **0.3** `VotePoll` (3 reads, 3 writes, 1 TTL delete later), **0.1** `GetPost` of a closed poll (1 read), 0.02 poll posts (a poll is fields on the post doc, +0), **0.02 GIF uploads** (CreateUpload + FinalizeUpload, 2 Vision units each, 4 Class A and 6 Class B per GIF), **0.3 GIF plays** (tap, full object about 1 MB from GCS, Class B 1) | the vote count and the GIF share are assumptions with no traffic behind them; the GIF share is carved out of nothing (additive), so if GIFs displace photos the Vision and egress deltas are an upper bound. The 2 s GIF decode deadline and the 1-GIF-at-a-time slot are per instance |
| W5 Part A call mix (2026-10-31 W5-18, ADR-0030 / plan assumptions, kept; reads re-measured in §17) | per DAU per day: 30% of DAU have a pinned list of about 40 members (C = 2 chunks of 30); **1.2** `GetListTimeline` refreshes (about 3 new list posts each) and **0.3** cold opens or older pages; 0.1 `GetList`, 0.1 `ListListMembers`, **0.3** `ListUserLists`, **0.2** `AddListMember`, 0.05 `RemoveListMember`, 0.022 create / update / delete, 0.02 `SetHomeTabs`; `GetMe.home_tabs` adds 0 calls | every rate is an assumption with no traffic behind it (the dev allowlist of W5-19 gives the first figures); the list-feed trigger needs about 2.9x this usage (§6). A refresh on a warm instance costs C + new posts; on a cold instance 9 to 11 + new (the model keeps 7 for the blend, the plan's 4 + new) |
| `[planned]` rows | engagement + notifications modules, not yet in `proto/` | included so the Phase 1 total is honest |

## 2. Per-RPC budget (Firestore reads/writes per call; worst from proto comments, typical used for totals)
| RPC | reads worst / typical | writes worst / typical | deletes (eventual) | Cloud Run ms (warm, est.) | calls/DAU/day | reads/DAU | writes/DAU | deletes/DAU |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IdentityService.GetMe | 2 / 1 | 0 | 0 | 15 | 4 | 4.0 | 0 | 0 |
| IdentityService.GetProfile | 4 / 1 | 0 | 0 | 20 | 3 | 3.0 | 0 | 0 |
| IdentityService.CreateProfile | 2 / 2 | 3 / 3 | 0 | 80 | 0.05 | 0.1 | 0.15 | 0 |
| IdentityService.CheckHandleAvailability | 1 / 1 | 0 | 0 | 15 | 0.15 | 0.15 | 0 | 0 |
| IdentityService.UpdateProfile (+snapshot job ≤ 100 posts) | 2 + 100 / 12.5 avg | 1 + 100 / 12.5 avg | 0 | 40 (+job) | 0.02 | 0.25 | 0.25 | 0 |
| IdentityService.ChangeHandle | 2 / 2 | 2 / 2 (+job) | 1 | 60 | ~0 | ~0 | ~0 | ~0 |
| IdentityService.DeleteAccount (+job; reference account P 300, O = I = 100, derived, `cost-report-account-lifecycle.md`) | 2 + job (P+O+I+...) / **513** (2 RPC + 2 gated + 509 work) | 1 + 300 + 1 / **302** (W3-26a D-2: +1 for the identity step's `ArrayRemove` on `privacy/protectedAuthors`; founder-accepted D-2 follow-up, W3-35a and W3-40a, same method as before: the reference account measured; 0.302 writes/DAU rounds to the same 0.30) | **505** | 30 (+ job about 10 vCPU-s) | 0.001 | 0.51 | 0.30 | 0.51 |
| IdentityService.RequestAccountExport / GetAccountExport (+job; reference account, derived) | 2 / 1 + job 703 + 3 polls = **708** | 2 + 2 (job: lease claim, status) = **4** | 1 (TTL, 7 d) | 30 (+ job about 20 vCPU-s) | 0.001 | 0.71 | 0.004 | 0.001 |
| GraphService.Follow (created; ADR-0008 A2: 4 cold / 2 warm) | 4 / **4 measured** (3.96–3.98 mean, T18) | 5 / 5 | 0 | 60 (server p95 6–17 ms on emulator) | 0.5 | 2.0 | 2.5 | 0 |
| GraphService.Unfollow (own 0 reads; the 1 read is the caller's status-interceptor profile read after Follow's `Forget`) | 0 own / **1 logged** | 3 / 3 | 1 / 1 | 40 | 0.1 | 0.1 | 0.3 | 0.1 |
| GraphService.GetRelationships (≤ 50 ids) | 1 / 0.5 planning (0.11 measured warm pool) | 0 | 0 | 10 | 3 | 1.5 | 0 | 0 |
| GraphService.ListFollowers / ListFollowing (page 20) | 102 (page 50) / **30.5** planning = mid of 20.16 warm and 40–42 cold (measured) | 0 | 0 | 70 | 0.3 | 9.15 | 0 | 0 |
| GraphService.Block | 3 / 3 (T16a) | 5 / 3 | 2 / 0 | 60 | 0.02 | 0.06 | 0.06 | ~0 |
| GraphService.Unblock | 1 / 1 (T16a) | 2 / 2 | 0 | 30 | 0.005 | ~0 | 0.01 | 0 |
| GraphService.Mute (3 reads after T26; 2 until then) | 3 / 3 | 2 / 2 | 0 | 30 | 0.02 | 0.06 | 0.04 | 0 |
| GraphService.Unmute | 1 / 1 (T16a) | 1 / 1 | 0 | 30 | 0.005 | ~0 | ~0 | 0 |

W3-22 M-3 note (2026-10-27): FinalizeUpload gained one write per image, the screening claim that stops concurrent calls from screening one image several times (`docs/runbooks/media.md`). The row above is updated (9 worst at n = 4, 4.4 planning, 0.66 writes/DAU instead of 0.4). The totals in this section and the figures derived from them in sections 3 to 5 are not recomputed: the delta is +0.26 writes/DAU, 0.8% of the 31.8 total, so no threshold moves. Fold it in at the next cost-model refresh.

W2 notes (2026-10-26 W2-15; emulator runs in §11, ADR-0023 "Cost impact" is the source of the call mix):
- **ListBookmarks matches the ADR: 43 and 103 on the first cold page, exactly.** Later cold pages of one open are 40 and 100 (the interceptor profile, caller graph and userLikes are cached for the rest of the open), so the per-open mean is below the ADR's flat 42 per page. Per DAU: 0.3 x 43 + 0.1 x 40 = **16.9** (ADR: 0.4 x 42 = 16.8). Warm (posts cached, inside the 60 s TTL) a page costs exactly the page size, 20 or 50, not "1 + page" as the proto comment and runbook say; "1 + page" stays the right ceiling when graph or userLikes have expired.
- **AddBookmark is 3 reads warm, not 5.** The 7 in the integration budget test is the cold ceiling and 5 is the replay ceiling; the planning 4 (ADR) holds (measured mean 3.06 with the target post and caller warm).
- **The no-op write (ADR dated note) does not move the per-DAU number**: the ADR's 1.4 writes counts every call as a success (4 or 2 writes), and a no-op is 1 write, so the no-op calls are an extra 0.02 calls/DAU at 1 write each, +0.02 writes/DAU. Writes: 1.40 to 1.42.
- Bookmarks alone (no quote): **+18.56 reads, +1.42 writes, +0.62 deletes, +0.82 requests per DAU per day**, against the ADR's 18.2 / 1.4 / 0.5 (+0.36 reads: the measured list +0.1, the no-op 0.06, the eraser and export 0.2; +0.12 deletes: the eraser 0.1, the no-op 0.02).
- Quote: 17 cold / 2 warm reads and 5 writes per `posts.proto`; the visibility check (+3 cold / +0 warm) is priced as the GetPost planning 1.0, so the planning read count is 2.2 + 1.0 = 3.2. The async quote notification adds 1 write and a TTL delete; its push-send read (0.05 reads/DAU) is not in the 1.3 notification line and is left out as immaterial. At 0.05 calls/DAU it adds 0.16 reads, 0.30 writes, 0.10 deletes per DAU. Rendering a quote or repost reads nothing extra (embedded snapshot).

W3 Part A notes (2026-10-27 W3-23; emulator runs and budget tests in §12, ADR-0024 "Cost impact" and Amendment 1 are the call-mix source):
- **What the implementations measured differently from the plan table** (the plan's Cost section is corrected to match):
  - `ListLikedPosts` is **23 cold at page 20 and 53 at page 50**, not 24 / 54 (the 24 / 54 are still asserted next to it as a ceiling). Later pages of one open are 20 / 50. A **warm** page costs the number of its posts that are not in the 60 s post cache (**0** when all are, measured; 20 / 50 when none), not "3". The planning 22 becomes **23** because a first page of an open is nearly always all uncached (the liked posts are old): +0.2 reads/DAU. Scrolling a viewer's whole 1,000-like list at page 50 is **53 + 19 x 50 = 1,003 reads** (50% of one instance's 2,000 daily read budget, INR 0.03).
  - **Pin clean-up is 2 reads per deleted post, not 1** (a fresh read that the post is gone, then `users/{uid}` in the transaction), 1 write only when the deleted post was the pin: +0.05 reads/DAU (0.10 instead of 0.05). ADR-0024 Amendment 1 A5 made the same correction. A message for a post that still exists is refused after the 1 read.
  - **`hasMedia` backfill writes every post that lacks the key**, text-only posts included (ADR: images only). It is a one-off: INR 1.3 per 10,000 posts (10,000 reads INR 0.33, 10,000 writes INR 0.99), and the 5,000-write cap per run keeps each run inside the 20,000 free writes/day.
  - `ListSuggestedUsers` measured **42 cold** (the cap, also at 600 followees: the cost does not grow with the number followed), **0 warm** inside the 60 s profile cache and **1** after it (the k6 build, before #350; **2** since W3-22 L-1, which re-filters every warm hit against the viewer's snapshot and reloads it once it has expired: +0.05 reads/DAU at the 5% warm mix, 35.7 either way); the curated path is **12** at the cap (the earlier "7" is the integration test's 8-id list of which 5 were usable). `candidates` is 10 on every cold call in the runs. **The 25 was a call-mix assumption, not a measurement; see the next bullet.**
  - Tabs: **24 cold through the chain** at page 20 and 54 at page 50, as the plan said; the typical call is **21** (the first tab call after the header's GetProfile pays the `userLikes` read), 20 after that. Page 2 of a 60-row Media list is 10 reads (the rows left). The viral-profile load (300 calls at 15/s on one author's Media tab) read exactly 20 per call: reads grow linearly with calls because there is no tab cache.
  - `GetProfileRelationship`: **4** on a fully cold instance in the chain (the "3 cold" is the service alone: caller graph, target profile, edge; the interceptor is the fourth), **1** right after the header's GetProfile (the edge), **0** inside the 60 s pair cache. The planning 1.0 reads/DAU is net of the replaced GetRelationships (2 calls x (1 - 0.5)).
- **Who to follow is the finding of this report.** The ADR planned 1 call per DAU at a flat 25 reads, i.e. about half of the calls warm. The W3-19 client keeps the result 24 h per account, so a DAU makes about one call a day, and the server's 1 h per-viewer entry is expired by the next day: a warm server hit needs a second call by the same viewer on the same instance within the hour (a second device, a re-login). So almost every call is cold. At 5% warm and 85% friends of friends (42) / 15% curated (12) the planning value is **35.7**, not 25, and the line moves from 8.7% to **11.9% of all reads**, above its own 10% trigger (§6). The whole restatement (+11.4 reads/DAU) costs INR 3.4 / 11.4 / 34.3 a month more at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU than the ADR's 40.2; the money is small, the trigger is the issue.

| Suggestions call mix | reads per call | reads/DAU | share of all reads |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADR-0024: flat 25 (about half warm) | 25.0 | 25.0 | 8.7% of 288.9 |
| **Used: 5% warm, 85% friends of friends / 15% curated** | **35.7** | **35.7** | **11.9%** of 300.2 |
| All cold, all friends of friends | 42.0 | 42.0 | 13.7% of 306.5 |
| Used mix with a 48 h client cache (0.5 calls/DAU) | 35.7 | 17.8 | 6.3% of 282.4 |
| Used mix with `SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` 20 (cap 32) | 27.6 | 27.6 | 9.4% of 292.1 |
| **2026-10-28 Amendment 2, decided: 48 h cache (0.63 calls/DAU) + edge limit 20 + B2 guards** | 27.6 | **17.2** | **6.1%** of 281.7 |

- The other rows move by +0.45 (tabs 21 not 20), +0.02 (banner Create + Finalize 6 not 2 reads per banner), +0.2 (likes 23 not 22) and +0.05 (pin job): +0.7 in all. The remaining +10.7 is the suggestions mix. Writes are 0.06 (UpdateProfile 0.03, banner 0.025, the pin write about 0), deletes 0.005 (the banner idempotency TTL), requests 2.29: both as the plan.
- **ADR-0024 Amendment 2 (2026-10-28; accepted #367, backend #373, client #374) restates this line; the table and figures above are the 2026-10-27 record.** The decided package is (a) a 48 h client cache and (b) `SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` 20 (cold cap 42 to 32), with the B2 client guards. Recomputed from the ADR's method, and it agrees: M = 0.95 x (0.85 x 32 + 0.15 x 12) + 0.05 x 1.5 = 27.6 reads per call; S = 0.6 x 27.6 + 0.66 = **17.2 reads/DAU**; Part A 51.5 - 35.7 + 17.2 = **33.0**; whole product 300.2 - 18.5 = **281.7**; share 17.2 / 281.7 = **6.1%**; the 10% line fires at 264.5 / 9 = 29.4 reads/DAU (1.71x of room). The decided row is added to the table above.
- The restated figure is a planning value, not a forecast: the call count, the warm share and the curated share are inputs with no traffic behind them. The first real number is the dev allowlist smoke of W3-24 (a handful of calls) and then `make cost` on the weekly actuals (§8).

W3 Part B notes (2026-10-28 W3-37; the k6 run and the `budgettest` lines are in §13; ADR-0025 "Cost impact" and its four implementation notes are the plan):
- **What the implementations measured against the ADR table, line by line** (planning values stay in the table; the plan's Part B cost table is unchanged):
  - **Follow to a protected target: 3 reads and 3 writes** in all 560 calls of the burst (the ADR plans 4 cold / 3 warm and 3 writes). **Withdraw, Block clean-up, the eraser and the export** are asserted by the W3-27 and W3-32 tests and were not re-run under load.
  - **ListFollowRequests is 41 / 20 at page 20 and 102 / 50 at page 50** (planning 42 / 21 and 102 / 51). The 41 is the caller's profile already cached by the GetMe that opened the screen; the 20 is the page's 20 rows, because the hydrated profiles are cached for 60 s. A later cold page of one open is **40** (interceptor, graph and the first profile read already paid).
  - **Respond is 2 reads after a List and 3.13 standalone** (planning 3 cold / 2 warm). The k6 burst answers requesters that were not on any listed page (the List showed the newest 60 of 500), so it is the **standalone shape the founder accepted at "up to 5"**: accept mean 3.49, decline 2.40, maximum 4. Writes and deletes match the ADR exactly: accept 4 / 1, decline 1 / 1, replay 0 / 0.
  - **The set refresh is exactly the ADR's rate.** Four refresh lines in 325 s, gaps 60.1 s and 60.2 s, and one refresh for 50 simultaneous first callers. ListFollowRequests, Respond and GetMe ran 100 to 190 s after the setup refresh and triggered none, so they do not consult the set; the read paths do (the Follow burst ran too soon after the setup refresh to show either way).
  - **GetPost is 4 cold for a stranger and 5 for an approved follower**: the 5 is the existing "+1 `userLikes` cold with engagement or bookmarks on" of the GetPost proto comment (the Part A pinned-post row measures the same 5), not a Part B read.
- **The two founder-accepted deviations are priced as allowances, not rows**, because neither has a measured call rate. Own copy (ADR note W3-29a, the same rule on own tabs from W3-30): +1 cold read when a caller opens their own REPOST or QUOTE of another user's post; the **assumption** is at most 10% of the 1.0 GetPost calls per DAU and 20% of the 0.45 Media / Reposts tab opens (the caller's own profile), so **at most 0.2 reads/DAU**, 0.07% of 282.95. Respond cold standalone (ADR note W3-28a): +0.005 reads/DAU at the measured mean, **+0.08 at the bound of 5**. Both together put the request path at about 1.51 against the ADR's 1.25 (+0.26, 0.09% of the whole product). **The request-count cap of 1,000** (ADR note W3-28a) bounds the `count()` at 1 read; the model already uses 1 read per call. **The `privacy_public` per-page read** (ADR note W3-28b) makes a 500-request job about 1,006 reads against the plan's 1,000 (+0.6%, once per account going public). **The notification cap** (200 per actor per day, 50 in the first day) only removes writes, so the cost can only fall; it applies when an account with hundreds of pending requests goes public. `AuthorSnapshot.is_private` (W3-25c) costs +0, measured on the six lists.
- **Lines not re-measured here.** The notification fan-out (0.18 reads, 0.09 writes per DAU) is the ADR-0017 planning value; the job under Pub/Sub push (the local API has no push wiring; the job was measured by the integration tests, 250 pending requests 503 / 753 / 250, and extrapolated); the eraser and export; the 5% protected share and every call rate, which are inputs with no traffic behind them. The first real numbers come from the W3-38 dev allowlist (3 uids) and then the weekly `make cost` (§8).

Graph notes (T21, all from emulator runs; `loadtest-graph.md` is the source):
- **Follow is 4 reads, not the plan's 3.** After a successful Follow, `directory.Forget(caller, target)` evicts both
  profiles, and `AccountStatusInterceptor` re-reads the caller. Measured mean 3.96–3.98 (max 4). The 4 is the cold
  ceiling; on this run it is also the typical. ADR-0008 A2 planned 3, so this row is +0.5 reads/DAU over the ADR.
- **Unfollow logs 1 read, not 0.** The RPC's own batch is 0 reads / 3 writes / 1 delete. The 1 is the caller's
  interceptor profile read once a preceding Follow evicted it. It is counted here because `fs_reads` includes it;
  it slightly double-counts against the identity rows (a profile read the caller was going to pay anyway).
- **ListFollowers page 20 is 20.16 warm and 40-42 cold**; 30.5 is the midpoint (the model's ~50% hit-rate
  assumption, §1). The 20.16 came from a 100-caller / 5-target warm pool, so it is a floor, not a forecast.
- Block/Mute rows are the T16a `budgettest` ceilings; they are not in the k6 run (0.02 calls/DAU, immaterial).
  Mute's 3 depends on T26 (not merged as of this update); today's Mute is 2 reads.
- GetRelationships measured 0.11 mean on a warm pool (worst 2). 0.5 is kept, the planning value.

Posts/timeline notes (T25, emulator runs; `cost-report-posts-timeline.md` is the source; reads exclude the interceptor unless stated):
- **Measured values** are from the existing integration tests plus throwaway `-overlay` tests (not in the repo). Cache hit rates, the 60 new posts/DAU
  and the call mix are still model assumptions: there is no real traffic.
- **Older page is 40 (mixed feed) to 42 (dense feed), planning 30: +33% to +40%, above the 25% line.** With 61 authors (self + 60) the
  first chunk is self + 29, so there are three chunks and k = ceil(2p/3) = 14; once the small chunk is covered by author-recent, two
  chunks run at k = 20. This is the ADR-0004 over-read, priced higher than the ADR assumed. ADR-0010 lever ("`2p` to `1.5p`
  when older-page reads > 1.4 x page size") has fired: 40 / 20 = 2.0. Not applied (no code change in T25); flagged for P9.
- **Home refresh overhead is 4 only on a fully cold instance** (graph expired, author-recent empty). Graph warm is 3, all warm 2.
  Modelled 4 because refreshes are >= 60 s apart and `CACHE_TTL` is capped at 60 s.
- **New posts cost 1 read each** (measured exactly), so 60 posts/DAU = 60 reads/DAU. The count of 60 is an input, not a measurement.
- **Settle re-read**: measured unit is 1 read per re-delivered post; the frequency (about 0.1 per refresh) is derived, not measurable
  without traffic. Uniform arrivals would give about 0.01 per refresh, so 1.0/DAU is an upper bound.
- **`userLikes` is out of every timeline row until P5** (no read, no cache entry).
- **F = 5,000 measures 168 reads (169 with the interceptor) against the 269 ceiling.** The seeded shape has 8 real authors; the ceiling
  needs every chunk to fill k. The ceiling is an assertion bound, not a planning value.
- Unit costs are exact across runs (deterministic emulator, no variance), so "mean" = the value.

- **Cold-versus-warm note.** The planning 3 reads is the cold-viewer, warm-post case (interceptor, graph, `userLikes`), which is also what every new voter on a popular poll pays; the all-cold 5 is the first vote of a viewer on an uncached post on a fresh instance. With 3 instances and scale-to-zero some votes are all-cold; if all of them were, VotePoll would be 1.5 reads per DAU instead of 0.9 (+0.6 reads per DAU, INR 0.2 a month at 3,000 DAU). The model keeps 3, the measured typical (3.00 to 3.10), and states 5 as the sensitivity.
- **The plan's per-RPC table needs no correction**; only its text for the GIF finalize writes (2.7 typical) is raised to 3. The ADR's Cost impact "VotePoll refused ... typical 2" is at most 2 and 0 for the author. The per-DAU lines: VotePoll 0.3 x 3.0 = 0.90 reads and 0.90 writes; closed-poll `GetPost` 0.10; a GIF upload about 3 reads (CreateUpload 1 to 2 plus Finalize 1 to 2) = 0.06; **1.06 reads**, writes 0.90 + 0.02 x (3 + 3) = **1.02**, deletes 0.30 + 0.02 = **0.32**, requests 0.3 + 0.1 + 0.04 = **0.44**.

Plan = `docs/plans/ui-w5-lists-communities.md` (Cost, "Per-RPC") worst / typical; ADR = ADR-0030 Cost impact (the as-built table, which includes the D7 idempotency document). C = ceil(members / 30): 2 for the 50-member list, 5 for the 150-member list (the `LIST_MAX_MEMBERS` default). "Within 25%" compares with the plan's typical; a measurement under the plan is within.

| `GetListTimeline` page 20, 50 members, non-owner, everything cold (viewer, list, owner, 50 authors) | 48 (6 + C + 2p) / 30 | 48 | **46** | **0** | yes against the worst; against the typical 30 it is +53%: the all-cold case, see the note |
| same list, page 50, viewer cold only (list, owner and authors cached by the previous call) | 108 / - | 108 | **58** | 0 | yes (below) |
| same, older page (`page_token`), everything warm | - / 30 | 44 | **30** | 0 | yes (exact) |
| `GetListTimeline` page 50, **150 members** (C = 5), non-owner, everything cold | 110 / - | 111 | **106** | 0 | yes (-4% on the worst) |
| same, page 20, viewer cold only | 50 / - | 51 | **43** | 0 | yes |
| `GetListTimeline` page 20, the **owner**, everything cold (no owner-graph read) | 46 / - | 46 | **44** | 0 | yes |
| `GetListTimeline` refresh, 0 new, warm, 50 / 150 members | 4 + new | 4 + new | **2 / 5** (= C: one billed read per empty chunk query) | 0 | yes (below) |
| `GetListTimeline` refresh, 5 new, warm, 50 members | 4 + 5 | - | **5** | 0 | yes (below) |
| `GetListTimeline` refresh with a two-idle-old `since_token`, viewer, list and authors cold, `FEATURE_REMOVED_IDS=on` | 3 + C + new (+107 past the removal-log window) | 3 + C + new + window | **11** (interceptor, list, graphs, `userLikes`, 2 chunks, 5 new, `removal_log_reads` 1, `protected_set_reads` 1); the +107 window query was **not** reached | 0 | yes |
| `GetListTimeline` after Home read the same 50 authors (page 50): list page 20, same viewer / another viewer | 44 | 46 | **0 / 3** (`authors_from_cache` 50, `timeline_chunks` 0) | 0 | yes (author-recent entries are shared with Home) |
| `GetListTimeline` refused: private list of another owner / unknown id / malformed id | <= 3 / 1 / 0 | 3 | **1 / 1 / 0** | 0 | yes (below) |
| `SetHomeTabs`, 5 tabs of other users' lists, all new, every cache cold | 7 / 3 | 19 | **11 and 14** (two runs; 5 on a warm instance) | **2** (`users/{uid}` field, idempotency) | plan's 7 corrected to the ADR's 19 (measured worst 14); typical 4 |
| `SetHomeTabs` replay (same key) / same content with a new key | - | - | **2 / 2** | **0 / 1** | yes |
| `SetHomeTabs` a 6th tab (FAILED_PRECONDITION) / reorder of 3 stored / the owner's 2 own lists / clear | - | - | **0 / 2 / 2 / 2** | 0 / 2 / 2 / 2 | yes |
| `GetMe` of a user with 3 pinned tabs, warm / cold; without tabs, cold | +0 | +0 | **0 / 2 ; 2** | 0 | yes (the tabs ride the profile read) |
| `GetList`, non-owner cold / warm / owner cold | 3 / 0-1 | 5 non-owner, 2 owner | **5 / 0 / 2** | 0 | non-owner worst corrected 3 to 5 (the ADR's); typical 3 |
| `ListUserLists`, own (5 lists) / another owner (5 lists) cold / with `contains_user_id` | 22 at n = 20 / 4 | 24 | **5 / 7 and 8 / 5** | 0 | yes (n = 5; the 20-list worst is derived: 1 + 20 + 3) |
| `ListListMembers` page 20 non-owner cold / page 50 warm / next page / owner page 50 cold | 24 / 2 + misses | 25 / 55 | **22 / 30 / 0 / 50** | 0 | yes |
| `CreateList` / replay | 4 (+20 at the cap) / 3 | 4 (+20) | **3 / 1**, replay **2** since W5-45 | **3 / 0** | yes; W5-45 L-3: the idempotency record holds the list id only, a replay re-reads the list (+1; asserted by the lists integration test, not re-run under k6) |
| `AddListMember` cold / replay / already a member / refused `LIMIT_REACHED` (the 151st) | 5 / 2 | 6 | **3 / 1 / 2 / 2**; since W5-45 **4 / 2 / 2 / 3** (a real add reads `users/{target}` fresh in the transaction, L-4; a replay re-reads the list, L-3; asserted by the lists integration test) | **3 / 0 / 1 / 0** | reads yes; **writes corrected 2 to 3** (list, quota, idempotency) |
| `RemoveListMember` / not a member | 2 / 1 | 3 | **2 / 2** | **2 / 1** | reads yes; **writes corrected 1 to 2** (list, idempotency); a replay is 2 since W5-45 (+1 list read) |
| `UpdateList` rename / unchanged | 2 / 1 | 3 | **3 / 2** | **2 / 1** | yes |
| `DeleteList` / again | 2 / 1 | 3 | **3 / 2** | **1** (+1 delete) **/ 1** | yes |
- **Cold-versus-warm note.** The planning cold open (30) is the case where the list, the owner and the viewer's graph are cached; the all-cold first open on a fresh instance is 46 at 50 members (48 is the formula's worst) and 106 to 111 at 150 members. With 3 instances and scale-to-zero some opens are all-cold. The model uses **38** (half all-cold, half 30); if every open were all-cold it would be 46 (+2.4 reads per DAU, INR 1.8 a month at 3,000 DAU).
- **The per-DAU lines.** Refresh 1.2 x 7.0 = **8.4** (warm C + 3 new = 5, cold 9 to 11, half each); cold open 0.3 x 38 = **11.4**; `GetList` 0.3; `ListListMembers` 0.1 x 22 = 2.2; `ListUserLists` 0.3 x 4 = 1.2 (n = 2 lists, derived from the n = 5 measurement); `AddListMember` 0.2 x 3 = 0.6 reads and 0.6 writes; `RemoveListMember` 0.1 and 0.1; create / update / delete 0.066 reads and 0.052 writes; `SetHomeTabs` 0.02 x 4 = 0.08 and 0.04 writes. **24.35 reads** (plan 22.1, +10%; ADR 22.4, +9%), **0.79 writes** (plan 0.51, ADR 0.79), deletes (the TTL on each idempotency document, plus the deleted list) **0.294**, requests **2.292**.
- **Every list query has a Limit and none loops over members:** the member fan-in is `homeSources` (chunks of `posts.MaxByAuthors` 30 under an errgroup of 4, each `Limit(k)`, k <= `posts.MaxLimit` 50; `timeline/service.go:144-166`, `posts/repo_firestore.go:384`); `ListByOwner` is `Limit(<= 51)` (`lists/repo_read.go:55`); the cap `count()` is `Limit(MaxPerUser)` (`repo_firestore.go:141`); the eraser and export pages are `Limit(500)` and `Limit(100)` (`purge.go:24-26`). The one non-batched loop is `SetHomeTabs` validating each new tab with a sequential `lists.Get` (`timeline/home_tabs.go:200`), bounded by `HOME_TABS_MAX_PINNED` (5); see the §17 findings.
## 3. Firestore totals vs quota (80% line = 40k reads, 16k writes, 16k deletes per day)
Whole product, including the `[planned]` likes, notifications and GetThread rows (230.0 reads, 31.8 writes, 7.65 deletes per DAU):
| DAU | reads/day | % of 80% line | writes/day | % of 80% line | deletes/day | % of 80% line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 100 | 23.0k | 58% | 3.2k | 20% | 0.8k | 5% |
| 300 (Stage 0 target) | **69.0k** | **173% (138% of quota)** | 9.5k | 60% | 2.3k | 14% |

**Finding (whole product):** reads cross the 80% line at **~174 DAU** (was ~210) and the full free quota at **~217 DAU** (was ~262). Stage 0's
300-DAU target costs ≈ 19.0k reads/day over → ≈ **$0.20/month** at the verified asia-south1 read price of $0.035 per 100k (2026-10-23 ADR-0022; was $0.34 at the old $0.06 upper bound, $0.13 before that). Still pay-per-use with no step change, but the
"$0 up to 300 DAU" goal is **not** met on reads (2026-10-23 ADR-0022: nor on the whole bill: Stage 0 is about INR 434/month at 300 DAU, §5, mostly Vision and egress) without the §6 levers. Writes stay free to ~630 DAU (was ~614); deletes to ~2,600 DAU.
Where the +39.2 reads/DAU since the 191 came from, row by row: older page 23 to 40 (+17.0), interceptor line (+10.6, new line), refresh overhead 3 to 4 (+8.0),
cold open 2.3 to 3.65 (+1.35), settle re-reads (+1.0, new line), CreatePost 1.5 to 2.2 (+0.7), GetPost 0.5 to 1.0 (+0.5). The ADR-0010 corrections account for +28.9
(162.2 vs the old posts rows); the measured values add +10.3 on top.

**W2 delta (2026-10-26 W2-15): bookmarks + quote UI, flags off in prod until W2-16 and the MVP ramp.** Whole product with W2 on for every DAU = 248.7 reads, 33.5 writes, 8.4 deletes, 37.7 requests per DAU per day (230.0 + 18.7, 31.8 + 1.7, 7.65 + 0.7, 36.8 + 0.9). Rows are the §2 W2 block; only the DAU at which the W2 flags are on counts.
| DAU | reads/day | % of 80% line | writes/day | % of 80% line | deletes/day | % of 80% line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 100 | 24.9k | 62% | 3.4k | 21% | 0.8k | 5% |
| 300 (Stage 0 target) | **74.6k** | **187% (149% of quota)** | 10.1k | 63% | 2.5k | 16% |
| 1,000 | 248.7k | 622% | 33.5k | 209% | 8.4k | 52% |
- **The free read quota is now exhausted at about 201 DAU (was 217) and the 80% line (the 40k/day alert) at about 161 DAU (was 174).** Writes: quota at about 597 DAU (was 630), 80% line at about 477. Deletes: quota at about 2,390 (was 2,614).
- **Bookmarks alone (no quote UI): 248.6 reads, 33.2 writes, 8.3 deletes per DAU**; the crossovers are the same to the nearest DAU (201, 602, 2,420).
- **ListBookmarks is 16.9 of 248.7 reads = 6.8% of the daily reads** at the model's usage, against the ADR-0023 trigger of 15% (§6). The share reaches 15% at about 37 list reads per DAU, i.e. 2.2x the modelled list usage (about 0.9 list-page opens per DAU a day instead of 0.4), with the rest of the product unchanged.
- Cloud Run: W2 adds about 0.87 requests and 2.6 vCPU-s per DAU-month (0.87 x 0.1 s x 30.42); the first Cloud Run limit (vCPU-s, §4) moves from about 1,603 to about 1,566 DAU.

Rows are the §2 W3 block; only the DAU at which the Part A flags are on counts.
| DAU | reads/day | % of 80% line | writes/day | % of 80% line | deletes/day | % of 80% line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 100 | 28.2k | 70% | 3.4k | 21% | 0.8k | 5% |
| 300 (Stage 0 target) | **84.5k** | **211% (169% of quota)** | 10.1k | 63% | 2.5k | 16% |
| 1,000 | 281.7k | 704% | 33.6k | 210% | 8.4k | 53% |

Deletes: quota at about 2,380.
- The prod alert "Firestore reads > 40k/day" therefore fires at about 142 DAU with every flag on (credit mode's 250k/day line, until 2026-12-22, at about 887 DAU; 833 before Amendment 2, 1,005 before Part A): expected, it is the pay-per-use signal, not an incident. No alert policy is added or changed here.
- **Shares of the daily reads at the modelled usage:** who to follow 17.2 of 281.7 = **6.1%** (trigger 10%: 1.71x of room, §6; it was 11.9% before Amendment 2); tabs 9.45 and likes 4.6, together 14.05 = **5.0%** (trigger 15%: about 3.4x the modelled usage, with the rest unchanged); all of Part A 33.0 of 281.7 = 11.7%.
- Cloud Run: Part A adds 1.9 requests and 5.8 vCPU-s per DAU-month (1.92 x 0.1 s x 30.42; 2.3 and 7.0 before Amendment 2); the first Cloud Run limit (vCPU-s, §4) moves from about 1,566 to about 1,490 DAU (1,475 before), requests from about 1,743 to about 1,660 (1,643 before).

**W3 Part B delta (2026-10-28 W3-37): private accounts and follow requests; `FEATURE_PRIVATE_ACCOUNTS` is off everywhere until W3-38 puts the 3 dev uids on the allowlist, no prod entry (prod apply on hold).** Whole product with W2, Part A and Part B on for every DAU = 282.95 reads, 33.76 writes, 8.45 deletes, 39.7 requests per DAU per day (281.7 + 1.25, 33.6 + 0.16, 8.4 + 0.05, 39.6 + 0.1) **plus the protected-set refresh**, which is per instance and not per DAU: 320 / 960 / 1,577 / 2,400 / 4,320 reads a day at 100 / 300 / 600 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU (§1; the 100 and 600 points are interpolated). **The refresh line is the one exception to "only at the DAU where the flag is on":** it is not flag-gated. `posts.Audience` is never gated and the loader is built unconditionally in `apiserver.go`, so the refresh is paid from the first revision that carries W3-26a (dev already serves one), whatever the flag says and with zero protected accounts. The ADR's "about 293 reads per DAU, free reads end at about 170 DAU" was computed on the pre-Amendment-2 base of 300.2; on the 281.7 base the figures are below.
| DAU | reads/day incl. refresh | % of 80% line | writes/day | % of 80% line | deletes/day | % of 80% line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 100 | 28.6k | 72% | 3.4k | 21% | 0.8k | 5% |
| 300 (Stage 0 target) | **85.8k** | **215% (172% of quota)** | 10.1k | 63% | 2.5k | 16% |
| 1,000 | 285.4k | 713% | 33.8k | 211% | 8.5k | 53% |
- **The free read quota is exhausted at about 175 DAU (177 with W2 and Part A; 201 with W2 alone) and the 80% line (the 40k/day alert) at about 140 DAU (142).** The refresh alone moves them by 2 and 1.6 DAU (176.7 and 141.4 without it). With both allowances at their upper bounds (283.5 reads/DAU): 174 and 139.5. Writes: quota at about 592 DAU (596), 80% line at about 474. Deletes: quota at about 2,367 (2,380). **Part B does not move any crossover by more than 2%.**
- Credit mode's 250k/day line (until 2026-12-22) is reached at about **876 DAU** (887 before Part B). The prod alert "Firestore reads > 40k/day" fires at about 140 DAU with every flag on: expected, it is the pay-per-use signal, not an incident. No alert policy is added or changed.
- **Shares of the daily reads at the modelled usage:** who to follow 17.2 of 282.95 = **6.08%** (6.0% at 300 DAU with the refresh in the denominator), against its unchanged 10% line: the line now fires at suggestions above about **29.5 reads/DAU** (29.4 before), 1.71x of room, so Part B does not touch the Amendment 2 headroom. Tabs plus likes 14.05 = 5.0% (15% line, unchanged). **Part B itself** is 1.25 + 3.2 = 4.45 of 286.15 reads = **1.6%** at 300 DAU (1.3% at 1,000, 0.9% at 3,000). ADR-0025 sets no per-feature share trigger (its triggers are structural, §6); the watch line proposed here, in the manner of the Part A rollback line, is **Part B request-path reads per DAU above twice the model (about 2.5)**, read from the §9 query, with no alert.
- **Abuse bound (W3-36, `docs/reviews/security-review-w3b.md` "Effective limits and cost bound", merged in #403):** a request ring of 100 accounts x 200 request-and-withdraw cycles a day is about 120k reads, 100k writes and 20k deletes, INR 420/month (1.7% of the INR 25,000 cap), and about 20 such accounts exhaust the free 20k writes a day; the toggle-and-job loop is at most 2 x 1,505 writes per protected account per day (about INR 0.3). The measured per-call costs here are at or below the review's inputs (Follow request path 3 reads / 3 writes against its 4 / 3), so the bound stands. The review's L-4 (a Home page consults the set for every author row) does not change the refresh: it stays at most 1 read per instance per 60 s, and 4,320 a day is the continuous-traffic maximum used above.
- Cloud Run: Part B adds 0.1 requests and 0.30 vCPU-s per DAU-month (0.1 x 0.1 s x 30.42); the first Cloud Run limit (vCPU-s, §4) moves from about 1,490 to about 1,485 DAU, requests from about 1,660 to about 1,653.

**Released scope (identity + graph + posts/timelines, after P1), the one that matters for the next release:**
20.4 + 172.5 = **192.9 reads**, 7.15 writes, 1.15 deletes, 24.45 requests per DAU. ADR-0010 forecast: 182.6 reads (**measured +5.6%**, within 25%).
| | ADR-0010 forecast | Re-based on measurements | Difference |
|---|---|---|---|
| Reads per DAU per day | 182.6 | **192.9** | +5.6% |
| Reads at 300 DAU | 54.8k (110% of free) | **57.9k (116% of free)** | +5.6% |
| Free quota runs out at | ≈ 274 DAU | **≈ 259 DAU** | -5.5% |
| 80% line (40k) crossed at | ≈ 219 DAU | **≈ 207 DAU** | -5.5% |
| Overage at 300 DAU | 4.8k reads/day, ≈ $0.09/month | **7.9k reads/day, ≈ $0.08/month** (2026-10-23 ADR-0022 price; was $0.14) | **+64% in dollars (+3.1k reads/day, +$0.05)** |
The reads figure is within 25% of the ADR. The dollar figure is not, only because overage is the small difference between two close numbers; it is 5 cents. It is
still inside founder decision D1 (pay-per-use), but the cause is one planning value above 25% (older page, +33%), so the k lever is flagged for P9 and the ADR-0010
revisit is triggered (`cost-report-posts-timeline.md`). At 300 DAU the identity + graph + posts scope costs 57.9k reads against a 40k alert threshold.
Writes: 2.1k/day at 300 DAU (11%), free to ~2,800 DAU. v0.2.0 (identity + graph only) is unchanged: 20.4 reads, ≈ 2,450 DAU.

Deletes 17%.

**W5 Part A delta (2026-10-31 W5-18): lists and Home tabs, flag off until W5-19 and the MVP ramp.** Whole product with Part A on for every DAU = **385.4 reads, 41.34 writes, 9.11 deletes, 1,875 requests per DAU-month** (361.0 + 24.35, 40.55 + 0.79, 8.82 + 0.294; 1,805 + 69.7). The base is §2/§3's 321.0 plus the W2, Part A (W3), Part B, W7, W4 and W6 deltas; only the DAU at which the `lists` flag is on counts. W5 Part B (communities) is a separate plan and is not in these numbers.
| DAU | reads/day | % of 80% line | writes/day | % of 80% line | deletes/day | % of 80% line | Part A alone: reads / writes / deletes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 100 | 38.5k | 96% | 4.1k | 26% | 0.9k | 6% | 2.4k / 79 / 29 |
| 300 (Stage 0 target) | **115.6k** | **289% (231% of quota)** | 12.4k | **78% (62% of quota)** | 2.7k | 17% | 7.3k / 237 / 88 |
| 1,000 | 385.4k | 963% | 41.3k | 258% | 9.1k | 57% | 24.4k / 790 / 294 |
| 3,000 | 1,156k | 2,891% | 124.0k | 775% | 27.3k | 171% | 73.1k / 2.4k / 882 |
- Reads were past the free quota at 300 DAU before Part A (§3, approved pay-per-use, ADR-0022); Part A moves the crossover from 138 to **130 DAU** and adds 6.7% to the read bill. **Writes at the 300 DAU target stay inside the 80% line (12.4k of 16k, 62% of the free quota)**: Part A adds 0.24k a day; the 80% line is reached at **387 DAU** (was 394) and the full quota at **484** (was 493). Deletes 17%.
- **Free-tier budget table (`free-tier-budget` §2/§6): holds in the sense `CLAUDE.md` sets for Stage 0.** No fixed fee, no step change, and no quota that was inside its 80% line leaves it at 300 DAU; reads were already over by the approved plan. The `free-tier-budget` §6 Firestore trigger (1.5M reads/day) moves from about 4,155 DAU to **3,892**.
- **Shares of the daily reads at the modelled usage:** list feeds (refresh 8.4 + cold open 11.4 = 19.8) are **5.1%** of 385.4 (the ADR's trigger is 15%: it fires at list-feed reads above **57.8 per DAU, 2.9x the model**; the plan's rollback prompt at twice the model is 39.6); all of Part A 24.35 = 6.3%.
- Cloud Run: Part A adds 69.7 requests and 7.0 vCPU-s per DAU-month (2.29 x 30.42 at the 0.1 s convention of §1; **the measured CPU of the API process was 5.0 ms per request** in a 642-request herd, §17, so 0.1 s is a ceiling); the first Cloud Run limit (vCPU-s, §4) moves from about 997 to about **960 DAU**, requests from about 1,108 to about **1,067**.

### Read budget as an abuse bound (ADR-0010 D5 as amended; posts-and-timeline T24/T25)
The read-budget counters live in instance memory, so every bound is **per instance lifetime**. Numbers are the ADR/plan bounds (derived, not load-tested here).
The measured per-call costs above stay under them: the largest single call, F = 5,000 home, measures 168 reads against the in-flight hold M = 269.
| Actor | Bound | Reads | $ at the §7 price (re-priced 2026-10-23 ADR-0022: $0.035 per 100k reads) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unverified (minted) password account, any RPC | 0-read verified-identity gate (`PROFILE_REQUIRED` / `EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED` from token claims) | **0** | $0 |
| One verified account, one instance lifetime | `READ_BUDGET_PER_UID_PER_DAY` 2,000 + in-flight hold 269 - 1 | **2,308** | $0.0008 |
| One verified account, a day, steady state (<= 3 instances) | 3 x 2,308 | **<= 6,924** (13.8% of free) | ≈ $0.0024 |
| Same, rollout day (<= 6 instances) | 6 x 2,308 | <= 13,848 | ≈ $0.005 |
| Same, idle cycling (<= 90 lifetimes/day) | 90 x 2,308 | ≈ 208k | ≈ $0.07 |
| **Deliberate instance churn (residual R2), ceiling** (<= 270 lifetimes/day) | 270 x 2,308 | **≈ 623k per day** | ≈ $0.22/day |
| Verified sybils (residual R1) | per account x accounts | ≈ 8 accounts exhaust a day's free reads | ≈ $0.002/day each |
Detect with `jsonPayload.limit_name` (`read_budget_daily`, `read_budget_inflight`) split by `read_budget_key`, and the `read_budget_spent` field on every request
line; levers are in `docs/runbooks/abuse-spike.md` (T26). The bound is a hard stop on reads only for the account; the cost is cents, not a step change.

### W3 Part A abuse and risk bounds (2026-10-27 W3-23, from `docs/reviews/security-review-w3.md`, W3-22)
Dollar bounds at the §7 prices, INR 95. The reviewer's limits are per instance times the 3 instances of the Cloud Run cap; the read-budget counters are in memory, so a new instance starts at 0 (same caveat as the table above).
| Risk | Bound | Cost |
|---|---|---|
| One account at the daily read budget on any Part A read RPC (tabs, likes, relationship, suggestions) | 2,000 reads per instance per day x 3 = 6,000 | **INR 6.1 per account-month**; a 100-account ring INR 607 (2.4% of the INR 25,000 credit cap) |
| Suggestions storm (10/min, 50/day per instance, so 150 calls/day; 2026-10-28 Amendment 2) | 50 calls x 32 reads x 3 instances = **4,800 reads** a day, under the 6,000 read budget, so the call cap now binds (was 150 x 42 = 6,300, held to 6,000 by the read budget) | **INR 4.9 per account-month** (was 6.1); a 100-account ring INR 485 (was 607) |
| Scrolling the whole 1,000-like list at page 50 | 1,003 reads (measured), half of one instance's budget | INR 0.03 per scroll |
| Viral profile: N viewers on one author's Media tab | linear, no cache: 20 reads per call (measured); 30/min per viewer per instance | 1,000 calls = 20,000 reads = INR 0.67 |
| **M-3 (fixed in #351)** concurrent `FinalizeUpload` calls for one PENDING banner each screened it (2 units per screening) | before the fix about 7 concurrent on one instance, 21 at max-instances 3: 14 to 42 units per banner attempt instead of 2. Now one screening per image: a per-instance single flight plus a preconditioned `screeningUntil` claim write (30 s), so 2 units per banner; only a screening that outlives its 30 s claim (Vision slower than its 10 s deadline plus the copies) can still be screened twice (`docs/runbooks/media.md`, spend check) | INR 2 to 6 per attempt; **bounded by the cap**: at most INR 1,283 a month at the Stage 0 cap (10,000 units), INR 5,558 at the credit-mode 40,000 cap (22% of INR 25,000). The harm is the cap spent early (12 to 36 accounts in a day), after which every upload is REJECTED or, under `unscreened_established`, published unscreened. That was the exposure before #351. **Now** the extra bill is the claim itself: 1 write per finalize (`2n + 1` writes: +0.005 writes/DAU for banners, +0.26 for all images, under INR 1 a month at 3,000 DAU); a call that loses the claim or joins the leader costs 1 to 2 reads and no Vision unit (`finalize_busy`, `finalize_joined` on the request line). The fix is on `main`, so the security review's condition before `media` or `profile_extras` widens in prod is met |
| **L-4 (fixed in #351)** `UpdateProfile` write flood (was: no daily cap, the whole `users` doc rewritten on every call) | `PROFILE_UPDATES_PER_DAY` = 100 calls per account per day per instance (`profile_update_daily`, counts every call) x 3 instances = 300 calls, each at most 1 write and 4 reads cold, and **0 writes** when the call changes nothing: at most 300 writes + 1,200 reads per account-day (before: about 6,000 + 6,000) | **INR 2.1 per account-month** (INR 0.9 writes + 1.2 reads at the §7 prices; was INR 24); 10 accounts INR 21; 67 accounts use the whole 20,000 free writes/day (was 3.3). A real user changes a profile a handful of times a day, so 100 is never reached; the cap is per instance, so the account-wide ceiling is 100 x running instances |
| **L-1** suggestions re-check of a warm hit against the snapshot | at most +1 read per warm call (measured at `16524df`: exactly 1, then 0; §12) | INR 0.3 a month at 300 DAU if every call were warm; INR 0.02 at the 5% warm mix |
| **L-5** one account steering the friends-of-friends window | no read change (the query is a Limit 30) | INR 0; a quality and spam signal, watched by `source=fof` with `candidates` at 3 or fewer (§9) |
| ADR-0024 Amendment 1 A4 retire step (planned, not built) | 2 reads, 1 write, 1 TTL delete, 2 Class A per replaced or removed image | INR 0.1 at 300 DAU and 1.0 at 3,000 (ADR) |

The `privacy.uids` fieldOverride removes the single-field index on the set doc, so it adds no index storage.

Vision and GCS: no change (text only, F1).

### W2 additions to the bill (2026-10-26 W2-15, priced at the margin)
Added to the table above, not merged into it. Each line is the extra bill with W2 on for every DAU, against the same bill without it, so a quota the product has already passed is billed in full and one it has not is billed only past the free line (reads are past the free quota at all three columns; writes pass it at about 600 DAU, deletes at about 2,400). Inputs: +18.7 reads, +1.7 writes, +0.7 deletes, +0.87 requests, +8.3 KB egress per DAU per day (the ADR's 8 KB of PostViews, plus the quote); Mumbai prices of §7, INR 95.

- The ADR's 10x column (INR 75 at 3,000 DAU) is low by about 22: it calls Cloud Run "not binding", true to about 1,000 DAU, but at 3,000 DAU the request and vCPU-s quotas are already passed by the product (§4: 1,786 and 1,603 DAU), so the extra 0.87 requests and 2.6 vCPU-s per DAU are billed (INR 21). Bookmarks alone at 3,000 DAU: 97.6. At 600 DAU the ADR's 15 is 13.7 here (writes are priced at the margin).

- **Against the ADR's first Cost impact (INR 27 / 47 / 242 at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU): +3 / +11 / +33 a month before Amendment 2, all of it Firestore reads from the who-to-follow call mix (35.7 not 25); with Amendment 2 it is -2 / -8 / -32.** Everything else is within INR 1 of the ADR (the GCS egress and Cloud Run egress use 1,024 MB per GiB as §4 does; the ADR used 1,000).
- **Against the INR 434 / 1,626 / 3,396 of CLAUDE.md's honest target and ADR-0022** (the pre-W2 baseline, which this table stacks on): with W2 and Part A on for every DAU the Stage 0 bill is **about INR 465 / 1,692 / 3,708 a month at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU (+7% / +4% / +9%; 471 / 1,711 / 3,773 before ADR-0024 Amendment 2)**. Every line is pay per use, there is no fixed fee and no step; the baseline numbers in CLAUDE.md stay true for the scope they describe, and the line is worth updating when the flags ramp (not part of this PR).
- Vision is the only line that is not linear: Part A banners are INR 0 at 1,000 and 3,000 DAU in Stage 0 because the 10,000 cap is already reached, at the price of displacing 304 / 913 units a month of post-image screening to `VISION_EXHAUSTED_POLICY`. In credit mode the cap is not reached yet (12,470 units at 1,000 DAU, 37,423 at 3,000), so they are billed. Part A is 4.0% of the credit-mode bill at 3,000 DAU (4.7% before Amendment 2) and does not move the INR 25,000 arithmetic of §10 (about +15 a day at 3,000 DAU with W2 included, against a line of 371; +17 before).
- **Amendment 2 against the ADR's own saving:** the ADR gives INR 5.6 / 18.7 / 56.0 a month less at 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 DAU (confirmed: 5.6 / 18.7 / 56.2 here) plus "about 7" of Cloud Run at 3,000 DAU. This model prices the 0.37 fewer requests a day at its own 0.1 s each (§2), which is 9.0 (1.3 requests + 7.7 vCPU-s); the call's own 70 ms would give about 7. So the 3,000 DAU bill is 3,708 here against the ADR's 3,710.

- **The plan's INR 12 / 58 / 480 is confirmed: 12.5 / 59.2 / 483.6 (+0.5 / +1.2 / +3.6).** The whole difference is the measured poll (1.41 reads where the plan had 1.2: +1.05 reads per DAU, INR 3.2 of the 3.6 at 3,000 DAU, the rest rounding). **The ticket's tolerance (the reads per call within +/- 25% of the plan) holds on every row: the worst is the poll at +17% and the eraser at +12%.**
- **The vCPU line is 54% of the 3,000 DAU bill and is the one number that is a convention, not a measurement.** Cloud Run bills request-based instance time in 100 ms steps (recalled from the pricing page; §7 does not carry the rounding line and it is to be re-verified at the next price check) and does not double-bill overlapping requests. At 3,000 DAU the product averages 1.8 requests a second (52.3 x 3,000 / 86,400), so a request seldom shares its 100 ms window and bills about 0.1 s whatever its 4 to 10 ms of server time; with a poll herd (20 polls a second for 300 users, the §14 run) the polls overlap and bill closer to their own time. The 5 polls per DAU are INR 104 of the 262 at 100 ms, INR 21 at 20 ms and INR 4 at the measured 4 ms. **The model keeps 0.1 s because the traffic that bills is the sparse, not the herd; the lower rows show what overlap would save and nothing is promised.**
- **Idle (0 DAU): W7 adds INR 0.** No service, topic, Scheduler job, composite index, min instance or Terraform resource; the fieldOverrides are index exemptions (W7-3). Pub/Sub, FCM and Vision lines are 0 or inside the free quotas. The credit window lifts nothing and the INR 25,000 arithmetic of §10 moves by about +16 a day at 3,000 DAU (483.6 / 30.42) against a line of 371.
- **Against CLAUDE.md's INR 434 / 1,626 / 3,396** (the pre-W2 baseline that these blocks stack on): with W2, Part A, Part B and W7 the Stage 0 bill is about **INR 479 / 1,755 / 4,205 (+10% / +8% / +24%)**. Every line is pay per use, no fixed fee and no step; the headline in CLAUDE.md and ADR-0022 is worth updating when the flags ramp (not part of this PR).
- **Abuse bound with the new caps (W7-29, #451).** One account at the daily caps writes at most: 900 sends (300 x 3 instances) x 3 to 5 writes, 1,500 mark-reads x 1 write, 1,500 accepts or deletes x 2 writes and 1 delete: about 7,300 writes and 1,500 deletes a day, INR 0.7 a day (before the cap the mark-read alone allowed about 259,000 writes). The read budget of 2,000 reads per uid per instance bounds the reads at 6,000 a day for a probing account (a non-participant probe is 1 read, W7-28). About 3 such accounts exhaust the free 20k writes a day, which sets the size of the spam ring that matters (residual R1 of §4).

- **Against CLAUDE.md's INR 434 / 1,626 / 3,396** (the pre-W2 baseline): about **INR 544 / 1,803 / 4,852 (+25% / +11% / +43%)**, every line pay per use. At 300 DAU W6's Vision line is 98% of its own cost and 10% of the whole bill.
- **Idle (0 DAU): W6 adds INR 0.** No service, topic, Scheduler job, composite index (two exemptions and one TTL policy, ADR-0029 D9), min instance or Terraform resource of cost; the credit window lifts nothing.
- **Credit window (ADR-0022, Vision cap 20,000 with `reject` until 2026-12-22).** At 300 DAU nothing changes (365 units, INR 52). At 1,000 DAU the cap is not reached (13,690 units demanded of 20,000), so the GIFs' 1,217 units are paid: **INR 173** a month, credit-funded. At 3,000 DAU demand is 41,070 units against the 20,000 cap, which is reached on day 14.8 of 30.4; the W6 increment is INR 0 (capped) and GIFs and photos are refused for the rest of the month. Nothing here depends on a credit-mode item after 2026-12-22.
- **Vision against `VISION_MONTHLY_CAP` (10,000, default).** Demand is 13.69 x DAU units a month; the cap is reached at **730 DAU** (802 without W6). Above that, the day the cap is reached is month day 22.2 at 1,000 DAU (24.4 without W6), 7.4 at 3,000 (8.4), 3.7 at 6,000. **From that day to the end of the month every GIF upload is REJECTED (`capacity`) for every account** (ADR-0029 F11 point 4, whatever `VISION_EXHAUSTED_POLICY` says), while photos from established accounts continue READY_UNSCREENED. So at 1,000 DAU users see "Image uploads are temporarily limited" on GIFs for the last 8 days of every month; that is the intended cap behaviour, not an incident, and the runbook (`docs/runbooks/media.md`, "GIFs") says so.
- **The finalize-deadline overshoot is bounded and small.** A FinalizeUpload that runs past the media handler's own 20 s deadline (not the 30 s Cloud Run timeout) returns the 2 reserved units to the counter even if Vision already billed the call, so real spend can exceed the counter by up to 2 units per such event. At 1% of GIF finalizes that is 0.012 units per DAU-month, 37 units a month at 3,000 DAU (INR 5); an abuser is bounded by the media quota (20 uploads a day, 5 for new accounts) at 40 units a day per account (INR 5.7). The cap can be exceeded by this overshoot but not by an amount that matters; the Vision budget alert (net, per project) is the backstop.
- **Abuse as cost (ADR-0029, re-read against the measurements).** `VotePoll` at the caps (30 a minute per instance, 300 a day per instance, 3 instances): at most 2,700 writes a day for one account, INR 0.27; the measured 50 a second on one poll is a traffic shape, §6. A GIF at the cap costs 2 Vision units like a photo and is inside the CreateUpload bucket.

**Firebase Hosting, shared-link visitors (W2-8 share, W2-9 path URLs).** A first-time visitor who opens `https://dzeroth.com/post/<id>` downloads the whole web bundle before anything renders; measured in headless Chrome against a local server mimicking Hosting (`flutter build web --release`, the CI flags, JS renderer; §11): **10 files, 5,937,835 bytes raw, 1,847,149 gzip, 1,453,193 brotli** (CanvasKit comes from gstatic and fonts from Google Fonts, so neither is Hosting transfer). The stale v0.1.0 figure was 0.75 MB per new visitor; the bundle is now about 2.5x that, 73% of it `main.dart.js` and 25% the drift `sqlite3.wasm` + `drift_worker.js`, which a signed-out visitor fetches too.

| 1,000 DAU (131 MB/day used) | **123** | 157 | 102 | 38 |
| 3,000 DAU (395 MB/day used, already over) | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
- Past the free transfer a visitor costs $0.15/GB x 1.85 MB = INR 0.026, so **1,000 extra visitors in a day (a viral link) cost INR 26**, and the quota is not a cliff. Free headroom is thin only at 1,000 DAU and above: about 120 shared-link visitors a day.
- The 4 MB per DAU-month web model survives the measured bundle only for up to about 3 prod web releases a month: web API calls through the rewrite 2.06 MB (30% of DAU x 37.7 requests x 6 KB x 30.42), revalidating `no-cache` files 0.15 MB, plus 0.3 x 1.85 MB for each release a returning web DAU re-downloads. Weekly web releases (4.3 a month) make it 4.6 MB (+15%, Hosting at 3,000 DAU about $0.43, was $0.16).
- **The knob if the headroom gets thin**, cheapest first: (1) open the drift database only after sign-in (a signed-out visitor then skips `sqlite3.wasm` and `drift_worker.js`, 0.47 MB gzip, 25%); (2) split `main.dart.js` with deferred imports so the sign-in screen is small; (3) hashed names plus `max-age` on the wasm and worker. The watch signal is Hosting transfer above 80% of the day (288 MB) in the Firebase console, no alert policy; none of the three is needed at 300 DAU.

FX: INR 95 per USD is an assumption (mid-market 94.955 on 2026-10-23); +/-5% moves every INR figure by the same +/-5% (INR 90.25 to 99.75). INR billing accounts pay Google's INR SKU prices, which can differ from USD x 95. GST (18%) is not included and its applicability to this account is unverified.
Prod Firestore weekly backups (ADR-0007) are the $0.04 column (about 2 x 0.5 GiB x $0.035); the earlier tables omitted them.
Fixed monitoring line (decision 2026-10-10, R4; the only fixed lines in this table, not DAU-dependent):
prod keeps 4 alert policies (uptime, 5xx rate, Firestore reads, C4 Auth mutations); dev keeps none of the 3 older ones
(gated off in Terraform). Upper bound at the published $0.35 per metric reference per month plus $0.50 per million points
returned: about **$1.05 to $1.20/month** for prod's 3 older policies, plus **about $0.39/month** for C4 (ADR-0011),
so **about $1.45 to $1.60/month** in total (applies only once alerting is charged, see below). Uptime-check executions are inside the 1M free executions per project.
Verified 2026-10-23 (ADR-0022, Cloud Monitoring pricing page): alerting charges start no sooner than 2027-09-01, and billing, quota and uptime-metric policies are not charged. **So the fixed monitoring line above is $0 until 2027-09-01** and the $1.45 to $1.60/month upper bound applies only from that date; re-check before it.
Nothing else in this table is a fixed fee; every other line falls back to $0 with traffic. With paid SafeSearch at 2 units per image the $5 budget alert (ADR-0007; INR 500 is about $5.26) fires at about 320-330 DAU on this table (was about 520; interpolated between 300 DAU at $4.57 and 600 DAU at $11.15). Raise the budget amount or accept the alert as the signal (config change, not an ADR); ADR-0022 adds the credit-runway gross budget (§10).

## 6. Levers, cheapest first (use before any Stage 2 ADR)
0. **Older-page over-read: k from `2p` to `1.5p` (ADR-0010 reserve lever; its trigger has fired, older page = 2.0 x page).** Derived, not measured: two chunks at k = 15 instead of 20 saves ≈ 10 reads per older page, so ≈ -10 reads/DAU (back to ≈ 183 released). Needs a one-constant backend change and a re-run of the timeline budget tests; flagged for P9.
1. Notifications list via `since` + client cache (like the timeline): −15 reads/DAU → reads free to ~250 DAU.
2. GetThread first page 10 instead of 20; lazy "show replies": −5 reads/DAU.
3. Older-page prefetch only after the user scrolls past 70% (never on open): −5…10 reads/DAU.
4. Like notifications collapsed per post per hour (update one doc): writes/like 4 → ~3.3 at scale.
5. Cloud Run CPU: gzip and binary proto (already), avoid per-request allocations in the merge; raise concurrency
   overlap by keeping max instances at 3.
6. Image egress: keep thumbnails ≤ 40 KB; never load `url` in lists.
Scale-up triggers remain those in `free-tier-budget` §6 (Firestore > 1.5M reads/day ≈ 6.5k DAU on this model, or bill > $30/month ≈ 2.4k DAU on the 2026-10-23 ADR-0022 table in §5).
- **ADR-0023 trigger (2026-10-26 W2-15): ListBookmarks reads above 15% of the daily Firestore reads, or the Firestore trigger above (more than 1.5M reads/day or a bill above $30/month), sustained 7 days, needs an ADR for the Stage 2 saved-list design** (a `bookmarks(uid, post_id, created_at)` table joined server-side, or Memorystore; ADR-0023 stage ladder). Modelled share: 16.9 of 248.7 reads = **6.8%**; it reaches 15% at about 2.2x the modelled list usage (0.9 list-page opens per DAU a day). Plan rollback line (W2 plan, Rollout): bookmark reads per DAU above twice the model, i.e. above about 37. Measure it with the query in `docs/runbooks/bookmarks.md` "Cost trigger" (Logs Explorer filter plus a `gcloud logging read` + `jq` share; `make cost` prints the raw lines but no share column) and §9.
- **ADR-0024 triggers (2026-10-27 W3-23; `stage-ladder` rows "Who to follow" and "Profile tabs", plan Out of scope): any one, sustained 7 days, needs an ADR before the next step** (the architect writes it; `sre-performance` raises it). The rollback line of the plan is separate: reads per DAU on the Part A RPCs above twice the model (about 103), or an error rate above 2% for 15 minutes on a new RPC.

  | Trigger | Next rung (Stage 2/3 target, not built) | Modelled value | Fires at |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **Suggestion reads above 10% of the daily Firestore reads** | a precomputed table refreshed by a batch job over a Firestore export (served at 1 read), then a ranking ADR | **17.2 of 281.7 = 6.1%** (ADR-0024 Amendment 2, accepted 2026-10-28, kept the 10% line; 1.71x of headroom, 2.1x in the best case; it was 35.7 of 300.2 = 11.9% before, the ADR's first 25 gave 8.7%) | suggestions above about 29.4 reads/DAU (1.7x the model) |
  | Tabs plus likes list reads above 15% of the daily reads | a per-tab instance cache, then Memorystore with the Stage 2 timeline | 14.05 of 281.7 = 5.0% | about 3.4x the modelled tab and likes usage (about 2.3 first-page opens per DAU a day against 0.65 modelled) |
  | The `free-tier-budget` §6 Firestore trigger: more than 1.5M reads/day or a bill above $30/month | tighter caching, then Memorystore or Cloud SQL | 281.7 reads/DAU: 1.5M at about 5,325 DAU (4,996 before ADR-0024 Amendment 2; 6,030 with W2 alone); bill: about 2,140 DAU (2,105 before Amendment 2; W2 alone about 2,300; baseline about 2,400), by linear interpolation of the §5 points | not before about 2.1k DAU |

  - **Resolved by ADR-0024 Amendment 2 (accepted 2026-10-28, #367); the 10% line is unchanged.** The first row had read as "fired" at 11.9% before any real user. The decided levers are (a) a 48 h client cache (#374; 0.6 calls/DAU) and (b) `SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` 20 (#373; cold cap 42 to 32), with the B2 client guards (+0.66 reads/DAU): 17.2 reads/DAU, 6.1%. Not chosen: edge limit 15 (1.25x of room alone), `SUGGESTIONS_HYDRATE_MAX` 6 (still over the line alone; it is the next lever, about 14.9 reads/DAU, 5.3%), curated-only below 10 followees. Read the measured share against 6.1%, not 11.9%, and not in dev (3 allowlisted users). Walk-back: the edge limit may go from 20 to 30 only by a reviewed config PR that cites the fill check (22.1 reads/DAU, 7.7%); above 30 needs an ADR. Gate before any ramp past dev: the limit applied in that project (dev done 2026-10-28; prod rides the next approved plan) and the app release with the client half.
  - What to watch (queries in §9; no new alert policy, no new log-based metric): `fs_reads` per Part A RPC (suggestions 32 cold / 12 curated / 0-2 warm, likes 23 / 53 first page, tabs 20-24 at page 20, relationship 0-4); the suggestions share of `document/read_count` daily; `source=fof` with `candidates` at 3 or fewer (L-5: a steering account or a sparse graph, not a cost change); `suggestions_cache_hit` (the 5% warm assumption); Vision units per day and the month's total against `VISION_MONTHLY_CAP` (`make cost`, `scripts/cost-vision.sh`), with BANNER finalizes against units (M-3, fixed in #351: units above 2 per ready banner now mean a screening that outlived its 30 s claim, not concurrent calls).

- **ADR-0025 triggers (2026-10-28 W3-37; ADR-0025 "Stage ladder", `stage-ladder` skill): any one, sustained 7 days, needs an ADR before the next rung** (the architect writes it; `sre-performance` raises it). None is near at Stage 0, and none has a new alert: each is read from the §9 Part B queries.

Unsupported people searches are watched the same way, with no threshold.

Measured: the run at the cap read 2,001 and took 128 to 699 ms on emulators; the cost is capped at INR 2.4 a month, the harm is a stale list.

## 7. Prices used (verified 2026-10-23, ADR-0022; sources and URLs in `docs/plans/credit-runway-cost-model.md` §1b)
Replaces the 2026-09-26 upper-bound prices from memory. Verify again before each stage change and after any Google pricing notice.
- Firestore **asia-south1 (Mumbai)**: $0.035 / 100k reads, $0.104 / 100k writes, $0.012 / 100k deletes (TTL deletes the same price, no free quota), $0.104 / GiB-month storage, backup $0.035 / GiB-month, restore $0.23 / GiB. Free 50k reads, 20k writes, 20k deletes per day and 1 GiB. (Was $0.06 / $0.18 / $0.02 / $0.18 upper bounds.)
- Cloud Run (request-based, asia-south1 is Tier 1): $0.000024 / vCPU-s active, $0.0000025 / vCPU-s idle min instance, $0.0000025 / GiB-s, $0.40 / million requests. Free 2M requests, 180,000 vCPU-s, 360,000 GiB-s per billing account, applied as a Tier 1 spend discount. Idle non-minimum instances and IAM-denied requests are not billed.
- Internet egress to India (Cloud Run, GCS): $0.12 / GiB, **always billed**: Cloud Run's free 1 GiB is North America only, and GCS's free 100 GB applies to egress from North America.
- GCS Standard us-central1: Class A $0.005 / 1k, Class B $0.0004 / 1k, $0.020 / GB-month.
- Cloud Vision SafeSearch: first 1,000 units / month free, then $1.50 / 1,000 units. **2 units per image** (full image + thumbnail, `VISION_SCREEN_THUMB=true`; verified in `backend/internal/media/service.go` `finalizeOne`). Cap-exhaustion DAU for a 10,000 cap = 10,000 / 12.17 units per DAU-month = about 820 (1,000 free units run out at about 82 DAU).
- Cloud Monitoring alerting: not charged before 2027-09-01; billing, quota and uptime-metric policies are not charged; 1M uptime executions free.
- Firebase Hosting: $0.15 / GB beyond 360 MB/day, 10 GB storage free then $0.026 / GB.
- Currency: INR 95.0 per USD (assumption, mid-market 94.955 on 2026-10-23), +/-5% sensitivity; GST 18% not included.
