## 9. Dashboard notes (Logs Explorer; no new log-based metric, no new alert policy)
- Graph reads by operation (T21): `jsonPayload.graph_op!="" | sum fs_reads by graph_op`
  (Log Analytics form: `SELECT JSON_VALUE(json_payload.graph_op) AS graph_op, SUM(CAST(JSON_VALUE(json_payload.fs_reads) AS INT64)) ... GROUP BY graph_op`).
- **Works since PR #36** (updated 2026-09-30): every graph RPC's request line carries `graph_op`, `outcome`,
  `txn_attempts`, `graph_cache_hit`, `edges_removed` (Block) and `feature_disabled` (flag rejections) next to `rpc`,
  `fs_reads`/`fs_writes`/`fs_deletes` and `limit_name`; a WARN `graph_txn_contention` fires when `txn_attempts > 3`
  (`backend/internal/graph/observe.go`; queries in `docs/runbooks/graph.md` §3). Fallback for log lines older than that
  deploy: `jsonPayload.rpc:"GraphService" | sum fs_reads by rpc`.
- Still **not** emitted: `rows_filtered` and `hydration_misses` (lists), and `lazy_removed` (until T27 ships). The purge
  lines `graph_purge_batch` / `purge_missing_counterpart` exist but are written by `opsctl` on the operator's terminal,
  not by the `api` service, so they are not in Cloud Logging.
- Existing alerts (uptime, 5xx, Firestore reads > 40k/day) are unchanged; the graph adds no alert policy.
- **Posts and timeline (T25).** Same form as the graph query above. Fields on the request line: `rpc`, `fs_reads`, `fs_writes`, `fs_deletes`, `posts_op`
  (`create|get|delete`), `outcome` (`created|replay|found|not_found|deleted|noop:not_owner|rejected:<reason>`), `timeline_op` (`home|user`), `timeline_mode`
  (`cold|refresh|older|gap`), `page_size`, `graph_cache_hit`, `timeline_chunks`, `authors_from_cache`, `items_returned`, `read_budget_spent`.
  - **Reads by RPC, Post and Timeline:** `jsonPayload.rpc=~"PostService|TimelineService" | sum fs_reads by rpc`
    (Log Analytics form, only if enabled; it is not at Stage 0: `SELECT JSON_VALUE(json_payload.rpc) AS rpc, SUM(CAST(JSON_VALUE(json_payload.fs_reads) AS INT64)) AS reads, COUNT(*) AS calls ... WHERE JSON_VALUE(json_payload.rpc) LIKE '%PostService%' OR JSON_VALUE(json_payload.rpc) LIKE '%TimelineService%' GROUP BY rpc`).
  - By operation: `jsonPayload.posts_op!="" | sum fs_reads by posts_op` and `jsonPayload.timeline_op!="" | sum fs_reads by timeline_op, timeline_mode` (split `outcome` for posts).
  - The T25 revisit signal: mean `fs_reads` where `timeline_mode="older"` over 7 days, against 40 (modelled) and 30 (ADR). Above 1.4 x `page_size` means apply the k lever.
  - Per-user spread: `jsonPayload.read_budget_spent` (never a body or text; `uid_hash` only).
- **Notifications fan-out (N-1/N-2 hardening).** The Pub/Sub push handler's per-delivery line (`message="notification_delivery"`) now carries `rpc="internal.NotificationsFanout"`, `fanout_fs_reads` (equal to `fs_reads`, kept as its own project-level field), `push_throttled` and `created`/`pushed`; `make cost` prints `fanout_fs_reads`. **Totals:** daily Firestore reads = `sum(fs_reads) + sum(removal_log_reads) + sum(protected_set_reads)` (the last is the detached protected-set refresh of ADR-0025, W3-37); `removal_log_reads` (the detached shared removal-log refresh on `since_token` timeline refreshes) is logged separately and is not in `fs_reads`, while `fanout_fs_reads` duplicates `fs_reads` on the fan-out line and must not be added again. `make cost` prints both columns. Fan-out reads: `jsonPayload.rpc="internal.NotificationsFanout" | sum fanout_fs_reads`. Producer-side drops: `jsonPayload.message="notification_fanout_capped"` (count by `actor_hash`).
- No new log-based metric or alert policy. The existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at ≈ 207 DAU for the released scope (≈ 174 whole product).
- **Bookmarks (2026-10-26 W2-15).** The exact `rpc` string is `/dzeroth.engagement.v1.EngagementService/ListBookmarks` (from the smoke log, §11); the three bookmark RPCs also carry `engagement_op` (`add_bookmark`, `remove_bookmark`, `list_bookmarks`), `outcome`, and for the list `rows_returned`, `rows_dropped*`, `rows_pruned`, `posts_cache_hit`, `graph_cache_hit`. ListBookmarks reads: `jsonPayload.rpc="/dzeroth.engagement.v1.EngagementService/ListBookmarks" | sum fs_reads`; all bookmark reads: `jsonPayload.engagement_op=~"bookmark" | sum fs_reads by engagement_op`. The ADR-0023 trigger share (ListBookmarks reads / all Firestore reads, a 24 h window): the numerator is the first filter; the primary denominator is the Cloud Monitoring metric `firestore.googleapis.com/document/read_count` (daily sum, it includes cron, job and fan-out reads); the log cross-check sums `fs_reads` + `removal_log_reads` over every line that has `fs_reads` (the fan-out `notification_delivery`, the account jobs and the sweep log theirs on non-`request` lines, so a `message="request"` filter alone under-counts), without `fanout_fs_reads` (a duplicate) and without the WARN `scheduled_author_reads`. The one-line `gcloud` + `jq` form is in `docs/runbooks/bookmarks.md` "Cost trigger".
- **W3 Part A (2026-10-27 W3-23).** Request-line fields (all counts and enums, never uids): `GetUserTimeline` carries `tab` (`posts|replies|media|reposts`), `page_size`, `timeline_mode`, `graph_cache_hit`, `userlikes_cache_hit`; `ListLikedPosts` carries `engagement_op=list_liked_posts`, `rows_returned`, `rows_dropped*`, `posts_cache_hit`, `userlikes_cache_hit`; `GetProfileRelationship` carries `graph_op=profile_relationship`, `graph_cache_hit`, `followed_by_cache_hit`; `ListSuggestedUsers` carries `source` (`fof|curated|none`), `candidates` (uids sent to hydration, at most 10), `suggestions_cache_hit`, `graph_cache_hit`; `CreateUpload` / `FinalizeUpload` carry `purpose` and `vision_units`; the pin step of the `post_delete` job reports outcome `pin_cleared`, `noop` or `refused`. The exact `rpc` strings end in `/GetUserTimeline`, `/ListLikedPosts`, `/GetProfileRelationship`, `/ListSuggestedUsers`.
  - **Daily shares for the §6 triggers** (read-only, once a day while a Part A flag is on in prod, dev for the allowlist; the same shape and the same denominator as the ListBookmarks query in `docs/runbooks/bookmarks.md` "Cost trigger"; the primary denominator is the Cloud Monitoring metric `firestore.googleapis.com/document/read_count`, this log sum is the cross-check; tested on the §12 emulator logs):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.fs_reads:*' \
      --project=dzeroth-prod --freshness=1d --limit=200000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload | select(.message != "scheduled_author_reads")] | {all_reads: (map((.fs_reads // 0) + (.removal_log_reads // 0) + (.protected_set_reads // 0)) | add // 0), sug_reads: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/ListSuggestedUsers")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), tab_reads: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/GetUserTimeline")) and ((.tab // "") == "media" or (.tab // "") == "reposts")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), like_reads: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/ListLikedPosts")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0)} | .sug_share_pct = (if .all_reads == 0 then 0 else ((1000 * .sug_reads / .all_reads) | round / 10) end) | .tabs_likes_share_pct = (if .all_reads == 0 then 0 else ((1000 * (.tab_reads + .like_reads) / .all_reads) | round / 10) end)'
    ```
    Seven consecutive days with `sug_share_pct` above 10, or `tabs_likes_share_pct` above 15, fires the trigger (§6); read the first against the model's 6.1% (11.9% before ADR-0024 Amendment 2), not against zero.
  - **Suggestions mix and the L-5 signal** (same `gcloud` line, then):
    ```
    jq '[.[].jsonPayload | select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/ListSuggestedUsers"))] | {calls: length, warm_hits: (map(select(.suggestions_cache_hit == true)) | length), cold: (map(select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false)) | length), fof: (map(select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false and .source == "fof")) | length), curated: (map(select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false and .source == "curated")) | length), fof_low_candidates: (map(select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false and .source == "fof" and (.candidates // 0) <= 3)) | length), mean_cold_reads: (([.[] | select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false) | (.fs_reads // 0)] | add // 0) / ([.[] | select(.suggestions_cache_hit == false)] | length | if . == 0 then 1 else . end))}'
    ```
    The model assumes `warm_hits / calls` about 5%, `curated / cold` about 15% and `mean_cold_reads` about 29.0 (0.85 x 32 + 0.15 x 12; 37 at the old cap 42); a mean above 32 is a regression (`SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` and `SUGGESTIONS_HYDRATE_MAX` bound it); `fof_low_candidates` high relative to `fof` is L-5 (one followee steering the window, or a sparse graph), a quality signal with no cost change. ADR-0024 Amendment 2 B3 adds two regression checks from the same query. Calls per DAU (`calls` per day over the DAU figure of §8, because request lines carry no uid) above 0.8 for 7 days means the 48 h cache is not holding (old builds, second devices, cleared web storage or a refill loop); the model is 0.63. Cold `fof` calls with `candidates` below 5 above 30% of cold `fof` calls for 7 days means the 20-edge window starves the card; walk the limit back toward 30.
  - **Vision units per day** (M-3): `make cost PROJECT_ID=dzeroth-prod` prints the month's units against `VISION_MONTHLY_CAP` and today's from `vision_units` on FinalizeUpload lines. For banners, sum `vision_units` and count the lines with `purpose="BANNER"`: more than 2 units per ready banner on average means repeated screening, which spends the cap early: since #351 only a screening that outlived its 30 s claim does that (`finalize_busy` and `finalize_joined` count the losers and joiners, who spend nothing). Expected banner units: 0.30 per DAU-month.
  - **Per-RPC reads against the model** (cold / warm): `jsonPayload.rpc=~"GetUserTimeline|ListLikedPosts|GetProfileRelationship|ListSuggestedUsers" | sum fs_reads by rpc`, then mean per `rpc`, `tab` and `page_size`; the rollback line is Part A reads per DAU above twice the model, about 103.
  - No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart: the existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at about 133 DAU with every Part A flag on (§3).

- **W3 Part B (2026-10-28 W3-37).** **Gap closed here: the protected-set refresh is not in `fs_reads`.** It runs on a detached counter and is logged as `protected_set_reads` on the request line of the call that triggered it (one line per instance per 60 s while serving). So **the daily total of the Firestore reads is `sum(fs_reads) + sum(removal_log_reads) + sum(protected_set_reads)`**; the Part A share `jq` above and the ListBookmarks query in `docs/runbooks/bookmarks.md` "Cost trigger" use the same denominator and now include it (the Part A `all_reads` above is updated, and the `docs/runbooks/bookmarks.md` cross-check carries the term in this PR). `make cost` prints a `protected_set_reads` column since this PR.
  - Request-line fields (counts and enums, never uids): `ListFollowRequests` carries `graph_op=list_follow_requests`, `graph_cache_hit` and `rows_dropped`; `RespondToFollowRequest` carries `graph_op=respond_follow_request`, `respond` (`accept|decline`), `txn_attempts`, `graph_cache_hit`; `Follow` carries `follow_outcome` (`requested|following`), `txn_attempts` and `target_uid_hash` (the per-target contention key); `UpdateProfile` carries `privacy_toggle` (`on|off|noop|quota|cap`) and `privacy_toggle_attempts`; the refresh line carries `protected_set_reads`; WARN `protected_set_stale` (`age_s`), ERROR `protected_set_unavailable`; WARN `graph_txn_contention` above 3 attempts; the job writes `privacy_public` lines (INFO per run, WARN when it stops early) and the fan-out `notification_fanout_capped` for the accept notifications past the per-actor cap. No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart.
  - **Part B request-path reads per DAU** (the §3 watch line, a 24 h window, read-only; the primary denominator for the share is still `firestore.googleapis.com/document/read_count`):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.fs_reads:*' \
      --project=dzeroth-prod --freshness=1d --limit=200000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload | select(.message != "scheduled_author_reads")] | {all_reads: (map((.fs_reads // 0) + (.removal_log_reads // 0) + (.protected_set_reads // 0)) | add // 0), set_reads: (map(.protected_set_reads // 0) | add // 0), set_lines: (map(select(.protected_set_reads != null)) | length), list_requests: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/ListFollowRequests")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), respond: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/RespondToFollowRequest")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), toggles: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/UpdateProfile")) and (.privacy_toggle != null)) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), max_follow_attempts: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/Follow")) | (.txn_attempts // 0)) | max // 0)} | .part_b_share_pct = (if .all_reads == 0 then 0 else ((1000 * (.set_reads + .list_requests + .respond + .toggles) / .all_reads) | round / 10) end)'
    ```
    The model is `set_lines` at most 1,440 per instance per day (4,320 with 3 instances) and `set_reads` equal to `set_lines`; `max_follow_attempts` above 3 is the Follow-contention trigger of §6; read `part_b_share_pct` against 1.6% at 300 DAU (it leaves out the GetMe count and the notification fan-out reads, which sit in the lines of other RPCs). The cold per-call values to read the means against: List 41 to 42 / 102 at pages 20 / 50 (20 / 50 warm), Respond 2 to 4 (5 at the bound), toggle 4 / 3, Follow 3 to 4, a stranger's GetPost 4 and GetUserTimeline 3 cold, 0 warm.
  - **Contention and the toggle:** `jsonPayload.privacy_toggle_attempts>3`, and Follow `txn_attempts` per `target_uid_hash` (`jq 'map(select(.rpc|endswith("/Follow"))) | group_by(.target_uid_hash) | map({t: .[0].target_uid_hash, n: length, max_attempts: (map(.txn_attempts) | max)})'` over the same `gcloud` output) for a viral protected account.
  - **The job:** `jsonPayload.message="privacy_public"`, counts per run; the cost to read it against is 2 reads, 3 writes and 1 delete per pending request (§13).
  - No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart: the existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at about 140 DAU with every Part B flag on (§3).

- **W7 messaging (2026-10-29 W7-23).** Request-line fields (counts and enums; never message text, snippets, uids or conversation ids in clear): the exact `rpc` strings end in `/SendMessage`, `/ListConversations`, `/ListMessages`, `/MarkConversationRead`, `/AcceptMessageRequest`, `/DeleteConversation` (service `dzeroth.messaging.v1.MessagingService`); `kind` is `send`, `list_conversations`, `list_messages`, **`poll`** (a ListMessages with a `since_token`), `mark_read`, `accept`, `delete`; `results` (rows or messages returned: the poll's `results` is how many messages it re-delivered or fetched), `folder`, `cache_hit` (the conversation copy), `conversation_hash`, `state` (`accepted` / `request` / `new`), `replay`, `silent`, `refused_reason`, `limit_name`, `txn_attempts`, `since_retired`, `entry_found`. The poll's `latency_ms` is an integer number of milliseconds.
  - **Poll share of requests and the poll CPU line (the stage-ladder triggers, §6).** Logs Explorer cannot divide, so take two counts over the same 24 h window: `jsonPayload.message="request" AND jsonPayload.rpc="/dzeroth.messaging.v1.MessagingService/ListMessages" AND jsonPayload.kind="poll"` and `jsonPayload.message="request"`; the share is the first over the second (the Cloud Run `request_count` metric is the primary denominator, this is the cross-check). The same in one line, `docs/runbooks/messaging.md` section 4 (tested on the §14 emulator log: 63% of all request lines, since that log is mostly the poll herd):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.message="request"' \
      --project=dzeroth-prod --freshness=1d --limit=200000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload] | {requests: length, polls: (map(select((.rpc == "/dzeroth.messaging.v1.MessagingService/ListMessages") and (.kind == "poll"))) | length), poll_p50_ms: ([.[] | select((.rpc == "/dzeroth.messaging.v1.MessagingService/ListMessages") and (.kind == "poll")) | .latency_ms] | sort | .[(length / 2 | floor)])} | . + {share_pct: (100 * .polls / (.requests // 1) | round)}'
    ```
    The poll vCPU line is `polls x billed seconds x INR 0.00228` (24 h, times 30.42): billed seconds are `poll_p50_ms` / 1000 as the lower bound (requests that overlap) and 0.1 as the upper bound (a request alone in its 100 ms step); read both against INR 300 a month (§6). Cross-check with the Cloud Run `container/cpu/allocation_time` metric for the revision.
  - **Messaging reads against the model (`fs_reads`, the same denominator as the Part A and Part B queries: `fs_reads + removal_log_reads + protected_set_reads`).** Per RPC, `kind=poll` and the contention signals, one pass (tested on the §14 emulator log):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.fs_reads:*' \
      --project=dzeroth-prod --freshness=1d --limit=200000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload | select(.message != "scheduled_author_reads")] | {all_reads: (map((.fs_reads // 0) + (.removal_log_reads // 0) + (.protected_set_reads // 0)) | add // 0), msg_reads: (map(select((.rpc // "") | contains(".MessagingService/")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), poll_reads: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/ListMessages")) and (.kind == "poll")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), send_attempts_gt1: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/SendMessage")) and ((.txn_attempts // 0) > 1))) | length), contention_warns: (map(select((.message // "") | test("^messaging_.*_contention$"))) | length)} | .msg_share_pct = (if .all_reads == 0 then 0 else ((1000 * .msg_reads / .all_reads) | round / 10) end)'
    ```
    **Read `msg_reads` divided by the day's DAU (the §8 figure; request lines carry no uid) against 38.05, the rollback line being 76.1**; the model's share is 38.05 of 321.0 = **11.9%** of the daily reads (polls 7.05, 2.2%). Per-call means (cold and warm together, one instance): poll 1.41 (a mean above 2 for 7 days is the cache missing across instances, not a code defect: the all-cold poll is 3), first page of 20 messages 20 to 24, inbox first page 21 to 23, send 1 to 7 (mean 2.4), mark-read 0 to 1. `jsonPayload.kind="poll"` in Logs Explorer with `sum fs_reads` is the console form.
  - **Contention (the ABORTED trigger of §6).** Logs Explorer: `jsonPayload.message=~"^messaging_.*_contention$" OR (jsonPayload.rpc=~"/SendMessage$" AND jsonPayload.txn_attempts>1)`. A WARN `messaging_txn_contention` carries `txn_attempts` above 3; `messaging_send_contention`, `messaging_accept_contention` and `messaging_delete_contention` mean the client saw `UNAVAILABLE busy, please retry` (`docs/runbooks/messaging.md` section 6).
  - **Writes per day** (the quota W7 pushes to 74% at 300 DAU, §3): the existing "Firestore reads > 40k/day" alert does not cover writes, so read `firestore.googleapis.com/document/write_count` (daily sum) in Cloud Monitoring, or `sum(fs_writes)` over the same `gcloud` output, against 16k before each ramp step. No new alert policy.
  - No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart: the existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at about 123 DAU with every flag on, W7 included (§3).

- **Search, trends and Explore (2026-10-31 W4-23).** Request-line fields (counts and enums; never the query, a tag, a handle, an id or a uid in clear): `rpc` ending in `/SearchPeople`, `/SearchPosts`, `/GetTrends`; `query_kind` (`handle`, `hashtag`, `unsupported`), `query_len`, `tab` (`TOP`, `LATEST`, `MEDIA`), `cache_hit`, `results`, `dropped*`, `fs_reads`, `code`, `limit_name` (`search_daily`); the refresher writes `trends_refresh` / `trends_skipped` (`fs_reads`, `fs_writes`, `trends_posts_scanned`, `reason`, `duration_ms`) and the WARN `trends_backlog`. Over one 24 h window, one pass (tested on the §15 emulator log):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.fs_reads:*' \
      --project=dzeroth-prod --freshness=1d --limit=200000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload | select(.message != "scheduled_author_reads")] | {all_reads: (map((.fs_reads // 0) + (.removal_log_reads // 0) + (.protected_set_reads // 0)) | add // 0), search_reads: (map(select((.rpc // "") | contains(".SearchService/")) | (.fs_reads // 0)) | add // 0), post_searches: (map(select((.rpc // "") | endswith("/SearchPosts"))) | length), post_unsupported: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/SearchPosts")) and (.query_kind == "unsupported"))) | length), post_hits: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/SearchPosts")) and (.cache_hit == true))) | length), people_unsupported: (map(select(((.rpc // "") | endswith("/SearchPeople")) and (.query_kind == "unsupported"))) | length)}'
    ```
    **Read `search_reads` / `all_reads` against 15% (trigger 1, modelled 10.5%) and `search_reads` divided by the day's DAU (the §8 figure) against 37.9, the rollback line being 75.8; `post_unsupported` / `post_searches` against 30% once `post_searches` passes 1,000 (trigger 2); `post_hits` / `post_searches` against 20% (trigger 4, modelled 40%); the count of `jsonPayload.message="trends_backlog"` lines (a separate filter: the WARN carries no `fs_reads`) on 3 of 7 days (trigger 3).** The trend refresh line: `jsonPayload.message=~"^trends_(refresh|skipped)$"`, read `fs_reads` (2,001 at the cap, 1 when `fresh`).
- **Lists and Home tabs (2026-10-31 W5-18).** `GetListTimeline` request lines carry `timeline_op="list"`, `timeline_mode` (`cold`, `refresh`, `older`), `list_members`, `list_authors_dropped`, `timeline_chunks`, `authors_from_cache`, `items_returned`, `cache_hit` (list doc), `graph_cache_hit`, `removal_log_reads` and `protected_set_reads` (flags on), next to `fs_reads`; `SetHomeTabs` carries `timeline_op="set_home_tabs"`, `tabs`, `outcome`. Counts and enums only; never a list id, a name or a uid.
  - **List feed share of the day's reads** (the 15% trigger; run daily for 7 days): `jsonPayload.timeline_op="list" | sum fs_reads` over the day's Firestore `document/read_count`. Log Analytics form: `SELECT SUM(CAST(JSON_VALUE(json_payload.fs_reads) AS INT64)) FROM <log view> WHERE JSON_VALUE(json_payload.timeline_op) = 'list' AND timestamp > TIMESTAMP_SUB(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), INTERVAL 1 DAY)`. Read it per DAU against **19.8** (rollback prompt 39.6, trigger 57.8).
  - **Reads per call by mode:** the same filter `GROUP BY JSON_VALUE(json_payload.timeline_mode)`, against refresh 7.0 (warm C + new, cold 9 to 11), cold/older 38, worst 111 at 150 members. A mean refresh above 10 is cold instances (or members posting heavily), not a code defect; a `list_members` above 150 with the default config would be a defect.
  - **Other list RPCs:** `jsonPayload.rpc=~"ListService"` summed by rpc against §2 (W5 Part A) and the 24.35 total.
  - No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart: the existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at about 110 DAU with every flag on, W4 included (§3); the credit-mode 250k/day line at about 690.


- **Polls and GIFs (2026-10-31 W6-21).** Request-line fields (counts and enums; never a choice label, a poll text, an id or a uid in clear): `VotePoll` carries `engagement_op="poll_vote"`, `outcome` (`voted`, `replay`, `already_voted`, `closed`, `author`, `rejected:*`, `contention`), `contention` (true when the vote batch lost 4 attempts), `txn_attempts`, `posts_cache_hit`, `graph_cache_hit`, `read_budget_spent`, next to `rpc` (`/dzeroth.engagement.v1.EngagementService/VotePoll`), `fs_reads`, `fs_writes`, `code`, `limit_name` (`poll_votes_daily`); a FinalizeUpload of a GIF carries `content_type="image/gif"`, `vision_units`, `media_ready`, `media_rejected`, `outcome`; each screened GIF writes one `gif_screen` line (`frames`, `sampled`, `units`, `decode_ms`, `refused_reason`; the trace joins it to its request line). **Since #530 the WARN `poll_vote_contention` and the `VotePoll` request line both carry `post_hash` (`logger.HashPost`: the first 16 hex characters of an HMAC keyed from `CURSOR_HMAC_KEY`, like `HashConversation`, so log readers cannot join votes to posts; an operator maps a candidate post id with `CURSOR_HMAC_KEY=$(gcloud secrets versions access latest --secret=cursor-hmac-key --project=dzeroth-dev | tr -d '\r') go run ./cmd/opsctl polls loghash POST_ID` from `backend/`, using the key version live when the lines were written), so the per-poll trigger is read from the logs (§16 finding 1, resolved).**
  - **Votes against the model, one pass** (reads per call 3.0 modelled, outcomes, contention; tested on the §16 emulator log):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND jsonPayload.message="request" AND jsonPayload.engagement_op="poll_vote"' \
      --project=dzeroth-dev --freshness=1d --limit=100000 --format=json \
    | jq '[.[].jsonPayload] | {votes: length, by_outcome: (group_by(.outcome) | map({key: (.[0].outcome // "none"), value: length}) | from_entries), reads_per_call: ((map(.fs_reads // 0) | add) / length), contended: (map(select(.contention == true)) | length), retried: (map(select((.txn_attempts // 1) > 1)) | length)}'
    ```
    **Read `reads_per_call` against 3.0 (a mean above 4.5 is the caches missing across instances, not a code defect: the all-cold vote is 5) and the vote count divided by the day's DAU against 0.3.** The W6 reads per DAU are `poll_vote` reads plus closed-poll `GetPost` reads divided by the day's DAU, against 1.06 (rollback prompt at 2.1).
  - **Contention (the sharding trigger of §6).** Logs Explorer: `jsonPayload.message="poll_vote_contention" OR (jsonPayload.engagement_op="poll_vote" AND (jsonPayload.contention=true OR jsonPayload.txn_attempts>1))`. Count by minute, not by uid; the contended polls are `gcloud logging read '<that filter>' --project=dzeroth-dev --freshness=1h --format="value(jsonPayload.post_hash)" | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn`. **Hot poll (the 1 vote a second trigger):** `jsonPayload.engagement_op="poll_vote" AND jsonPayload.outcome="voted"`, counted per `jsonPayload.post_hash` per minute; 300 `voted` lines with one hash in 5 minutes is the trigger.
  - **GIF finalize, the W6-22 smoke queries (ADR-0029 N4 founder decisions 1 and 2).** One pass over `gif_screen` and the GIF FinalizeUpload request lines, joined on the trace (tested on a synthetic log with all five cases; run it on `--freshness=1h` after the smoke):
    ```
    gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND resource.labels.service_name="api" AND (jsonPayload.message="gif_screen" OR (jsonPayload.message="request" AND jsonPayload.rpc="/dzeroth.media.v1.MediaService/FinalizeUpload" AND jsonPayload.content_type="image/gif"))' \
      --project=dzeroth-dev --freshness=1h --limit=10000 --format=json \
    | jq '(map(select(.jsonPayload.message == "request")) | map({key: .trace, value: .jsonPayload}) | from_entries) as $fin
      | [.[] | select(.jsonPayload.message == "gif_screen") | . as $g | {reason: ($g.jsonPayload.refused_reason // ""), decode_ms: $g.jsonPayload.decode_ms, units: $g.jsonPayload.units, code: ($fin[$g.trace].code // "no_request_line"), rejected: (($fin[$g.trace].media_rejected // 0) > 0), outcome: ($fin[$g.trace].outcome // "")}]
      | {gif_screens: length, units_spent: (map(.units) | add), decode_ms_max: (map(.decode_ms) | max),
         deadline_lines: (map(select(.reason == "deadline")) | length),
         deadline_rejected_gifs: (map(select(.reason == "deadline" and .rejected)) | length),
         deadline_but_not_rejected: (map(select(.reason == "deadline" and (.rejected | not))) | length),
         slot_busy_retries: (map(select(.reason == "" and .units == 0 and .decode_ms == 0 and .outcome == "rejected:upload_incomplete")) | length),
         deadline_exceeded_requests: (map(select(.code == "deadline_exceeded" or .code == "unavailable")) | length)}'
    ```
    - **Decision 1 (`GIF_MAX_FRAME_AREA_SUM` stays 50M):** the figure is `deadline_rejected_gifs` over `gif_screens`. Count only the lines whose request ended REJECTED (`rejected` true): the sampler logs `refused_reason=deadline` for any end of its context, so a client that hung up mid-sampling or the 20 s handler deadline shows `deadline_but_not_rejected` instead, and nothing was refused (N1, "Cancellation during sampling"). A non-zero `deadline_rejected_gifs` on a legitimate GIF reopens the decision. Also read `decode_ms_max` against the 2,000 ms deadline: a max above about 1,500 on ordinary GIFs is the early warning.
    - **Decision 2 (accept the rare retry):** `deadline_exceeded_requests` (FinalizeUpload ending `deadline_exceeded` or `unavailable`) and `slot_busy_retries` (the slot wait ended with MEDIA_NOT_READY: `gif_screen` with no frames, 0 units and 0 ms, and `outcome="rejected:upload_incomplete"` on its request; that outcome is shared with a genuinely incomplete upload, which has no `gif_screen` line). Any non-zero value is the evidence that reopens the 20 s deadline; the first lever is raising the deadline for a GIF finalize, kept under 30 s.
    - **GIF Vision spend:** `units_spent` (2 per screened GIF, 0 for a refusal or a busy slot), cross-checked with `jsonPayload.vision_units` summed over the same FinalizeUpload lines and with `admin/vision-{yyyymm}`; the counter can run up to 2 units under the real spend per deadline event.
  - **GIF share of Vision and egress:** FinalizeUpload lines with `content_type="image/gif"` over all FinalizeUpload lines (the model: 10% of images), and the Cloud Storage `network/sent_bytes_count` for the media bucket in Cloud Monitoring against the 58.0 MB per DAU-month (GIF plays are 15.7% of it).
  - No new log-based metric, alert policy or dashboard chart: the existing "reads > 40k/day" alert fires at about 110 DAU with every flag on, W6 included (§3); the credit-mode 250k/day line at about 690.

## 10. Daily gross burn against the credit-runway line (2026-10-23 ADR-0022)
> Amendment 1 of ADR-0022 (2026-10-26): the cap is now 25,000 (INR 403/day to the pause, 391/day to 12-26). The arithmetic below keeps the 23,000 figure.

ADR-0022 funds a bounded window from the Google credit: `credit_spend_cap_inr` = INR 23,000 from 2026-10-23, paused on 2026-12-24 unless a RESUMED ADR is approved (cap formula `min(23,000, remaining credit - 2,000)`).
- **Straight line to the budget end (2026-12-26, 64 days):** 23,000 / 64 = **INR 359 per day** (359.4).
- **Straight line to the planned pause (2026-12-24, 62 days):** 23,000 / 62 = **INR 371 per day** (371.0). This is the line the ADR and `credit-runway-cost-model.md` §4 use; the cumulative line on day N is 371 x N.
- **Expected burn (credit mode, model):** about INR 31 per day at ~0 DAU, 45 at 300 DAU, 94 at 1,000 DAU, 267 at 3,000 DAU (cumulative 71% of the cap at 3,000 DAU by 12-24). Stage 0 as configured: 14 / 53 / 112 per day. Anything above the line means a knob or traffic is wrong, not that the plan is working.
- **How to read it (2 minutes a day, founder or sre-performance):** Cloud Console > Billing > Reports, billing account scope (not a project), date range 2026-10-23 to today, **Group by SKU**, in the Credits filter untick everything so the view is gross (promotional credit must not be subtracted, otherwise every figure reads INR 0). Currency INR. Read the cumulative total and divide by days elapsed, then compare with 371 x day. The SKUs that explain a miss: Cloud Run `CPU Allocation Time` / `Memory Allocation Time` and the min-instance (idle) SKUs, `Cloud Vision` SafeSearch units, Firestore `Read Operations` / `Write Operations` in Mumbai, `Networking` internet egress, Cloud Storage `Class B Operations`. Billing data lags by hours to more than a day, so the fast signals are the request-count alert and `make cost` (below), not this report.
- **Vision units:** `make cost PROJECT_ID=dzeroth-prod` prints units used this month (Firestore `admin/vision-YYYYMM` field `units`, UTC month) against `VISION_MONTHLY_CAP`, and units today (sum of `vision_units` on FinalizeUpload request lines since 00:00 UTC). Without gcloud/Firestore credentials it prints `needs gcloud/Firestore credentials` and exits 0 (`scripts/cost-vision.sh`). Each unit past the first 1,000 a month is INR 0.1425 (1.50 USD per 1,000 x 95).
- **Thresholds** on the credit budget are 10 / 25 / 50 / 75 / 90 / 100% of the cap (2026-10-29, 11-07, 11-23, 12-08, 12-17, 12-24 on the straight line); an alert that arrives earlier than its date is the trigger to look the same day (`docs/adr/0022-credit-funded-runway.md`).
- Weekly: append the cumulative gross burn, the line value for that day and the top 3 SKUs to §8 (Bill column).

## 11. W2-15 measurements (2026-10-26, emulators and a local build only; nothing was run against a cloud project)
Machine: Windows 11, 16 logical processors (Intel family 6 model 154), Go 1.26 API binary, k6 2.2.0, Firebase emulators (Firestore, Auth, Storage) on the `firebase.graph-loadtest.json` ports, headless Chrome 156. Emulator latency is indicative only.

### ListBookmarks smoke (`make loadtest SCENARIO=engagement_bookmarks_list`)
Scenario `loadtest/engagement_bookmarks_list.js`, reads from the API request log with `node loadtest/analyze_bookmarks.js api.log`. Setup: 10 viewers (5 pairs), 50 authors x 20 posts, each viewer bookmarks its own 100 posts (disjoint sets, so no viewer warms another's post cache), then 75 s idle (CACHE_TTL 60 s). Then each viewer pages its whole list twice (the first pass is cold, the second warm: even viewers at page size 20, odd viewers at 50), then 4 warm passes/s for 30 s over the 10 viewers.
API environment for the recorded run (nothing else changed from defaults): `FEATURE_POSTS=on FEATURE_BOOKMARKS=on FEATURE_ENGAGEMENT=on`, `RATE_LIMIT_PER_IP_PER_MIN=1000000`, `RATE_LIMIT_PRE_AUTH_IP_PER_MIN=1000000`, `RATE_LIMIT_POST_CREATE_PER_MIN=1000`, `QUOTA_NEW_ACCOUNT_POSTS_PER_DAY=1000`, `RATE_LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_PER_MIN=1000`, `QUOTA_NEW_ACCOUNT_BOOKMARKS_PER_DAY=1000`, `QUOTA_BOOKMARKS_PER_DAY=1000`, `RATE_LIMIT_BOOKMARKS_LIST_PER_MIN=100000`, `READ_BUDGET_PER_UID_PER_DAY=10000000` (the budget run keeps the default 2000); k6 `PAIRS=5 RATE=4`.
| Case (100 bookmarks per viewer) | calls | `fs_reads` | server ms p50 / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|
| page 20, cold, first page | 5 | **43** (43 every time) | 174 / 179 |
| page 20, cold, later pages (4 per pass) | 20 | **40** | 25 / 43 |
| page 20, cold, terminal empty page (100 is an exact multiple) | 5 | 1 | 9 / 13 |
| page 20, warm, first + later pages | 66 + 264 | **20** | 4 / 16 and 4 / 11 |
| page 20, warm, terminal empty page | 66 | 1 | 3 / 6 |
| page 50, cold, first page | 5 | **103** | 176 / 177 |
| page 50, cold, second page | 5 | **100** | 46 / 54 |
| page 50, cold, terminal empty page | 5 | 1 | 13 / 23 |
| page 50, warm, first + second page | 65 + 65 | **50** | 4 / 17 and 4 / 12 |
| page 50, warm, terminal empty page | 65 | 1 | 3 / 12 |
- Writes 0 on every call (nothing to prune), 0 ERROR lines, 0 failed requests, 141 of 141 k6 checks passed (every pass lists all 100 rows once).
- k6 client-side latency (includes the loopback): page 20 cold p50 34.8 ms / p95 185 ms (30 calls), page 50 cold 49.9 / 186 ms (15 calls), page 20 warm 4.9 / 13.9 ms, page 50 warm 5.5 / 22.7 ms. All under the 400 ms Stage 0 target; the cold first page is the slow one because it does 43 or 103 emulator reads.
- **The per-uid daily read budget is charged.** `read_budget_spent` rose by exactly `fs_reads` between consecutive requests of the same viewer in 626 of 626 pairs. Budget run (default `READ_BUDGET_PER_UID_PER_DAY` 2000): one viewer seeded 100 bookmarks (308 reads, 100 AddBookmark), then paged at 50: 103, then 50 per page; the call that crossed 2000 was served (spent 2,011) and the next one answered 429 `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` with `limit=read_budget_daily` at 0 reads (the 34th list call; 33 pages = 1,703 list reads). So a user at the 1,000 cap reading 50-row cold pages exhausts the day's budget in about 19 pages, or about 100 pages of 20 once the posts are warm.
- AddBookmark in the same run (seeding, 1,000 calls): 3 reads in 950 calls, 4 in 40, 5 in 10 (mean 3.06), 4 writes each. CreatePost in setup: 2 or 3 reads.

### Web bundle for a first-time shared-link visitor
`flutter build web --release --dart-define=USE_EMULATORS=false --dart-define=FIREBASE_ENV=dev --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=/api` (the deploy-dev flags; Flutter 3.47.5, the CI pin, revision 6a19cca564; built from `main` at 46b9e0f, which has **neither W2-8 (`share_plus`) nor W2-9 (path URLs)**, so web share adds an unmeasured delta to `main.dart.js` and the `/post/<id>` load is otherwise unchanged; dart2js, CanvasKit renderer from gstatic; 81 s). The fetch list is what a fresh Chrome profile requested from a static server over `app/build/web` that rewrites unknown paths to `index.html` (as Hosting does) for 25 s on `/post/1234567890123456789`; anything not requested from that server (CanvasKit from `www.gstatic.com`, Google Fonts) is not Hosting transfer. The service worker is Flutter's self-unregistering stub, so there is no precache. Sizes by Node `zlib` (gzip level 6, brotli quality 11); request and response headers (about 4 KB over 10 requests) are not counted.
| File | raw | gzip | brotli |
|---|---|---|---|
| `main.dart.js` | 4,779,804 | 1,355,678 | 1,029,239 |
| `sqlite3.wasm` (drift, fetched at start-up even signed out) | 748,686 | 360,168 | 314,419 |
| `drift_worker.js` | 370,672 | 109,598 | 89,964 |
| `assets/fonts/MaterialIcons-Regular.otf` (tree-shaken) | 16,796 | 9,323 | 8,303 |
| `flutter_bootstrap.js` | 13,093 | 5,178 | 4,478 |
| `icons/Icon-192.png` | 5,292 | 5,213 | 5,170 |
| `index.html` (the rewritten `/post/<id>`) | 1,548 | 763 | 532 |
| `manifest.json`, `favicon.png`, `assets/FontManifest.json` | 1,944 | 1,228 | 1,088 |
| **Total, 10 files** | **5,937,835** | **1,847,149** | **1,453,193** |
Not counted: `canvaskit/` (25 MB in the build, deployed to Hosting but never requested: `flutter_bootstrap.js` loads CanvasKit from gstatic), `flutter.js` (not requested), `NOTICES`. `drift_worker.js` was requested twice by the browser against a server with no cache headers; it is counted once. Whether Hosting compresses `.wasm` and `.otf` was not verified (docs say it compresses with gzip or brotli when the browser accepts it); the §5 table therefore also shows the gzip-text, raw-`.wasm` case (2,235,667 bytes). A returning visitor sends conditional GETs for the `no-cache` files (`index.html`, `main.dart.js`, `flutter_bootstrap.js`, per `firebase.json`) and re-downloads `main.dart.js` only after a prod web release.

### What was not measured
- A signed-in session's web transfer (sign-in needs real Firebase Auth); the 4 MB per DAU-month line is still the model's, with the 6 KB per API call the doc has used.
- Cold-start and Cloud Run CPU for the bookmark RPCs (emulators only; the Cloud Run ms column is an estimate), and the instance cache hit rate (no traffic).
- Add/Remove/List on a cold Firestore instance in the cloud; the dev smoke after the W2-16 apply (a handful of reads) is the first real number.

## 12. W3-23 measurements (2026-10-27, emulators and the repository's own budget tests only; nothing was run against a cloud project)
Machine: Windows 11, 16 logical processors, Go 1.27.0 toolchain building the API from `main` fcd0145 (W3-4 to W3-19 merged), k6 2.2.0, Node for the analyzer, firebase-tools 15.31.0 with Firestore, Auth, Pub/Sub and Storage on the alternate ports of `firebase.graph-loadtest.json` (18080 / 19099 / 18085 / 19199) and the API on :18081, so another session's suite on 8080 was never touched; torn down afterwards. Emulator latency is indicative only.

**Build, and what was re-checked afterwards.** The k6 runs below were taken on `main` fcd0145. PR #350 (L-1, L-5) and PR #351 (M-3, L-4) merged afterwards (`main` 16524df), so the k6 figures are not re-measured for them. The three budget tests they change were re-run on the emulators at 16524df on other ports (Firestore 18180, Auth 19199, Pub/Sub 18185, Storage 19299, hub 14401; torn down afterwards, no other session's emulator touched): `TestIntegration_BannerBudgetsAndResolver` (CreateUpload BANNER 2 reads / 3 writes; FinalizeUpload BANNER 2 reads and exactly **3 writes**: the claim, the resolve and the Vision counter; 2 before M-3), `TestListSuggestedUsers_WarmHitDropsABlockerAddedElsewhere` (a warm hit with the 60 s snapshot expired: **1 read** plus the interceptor, then 0) and `TestUpdateProfile_Integration_NoOpSaveWritesNothing` (an identical save: within 3 reads with a banner and a pin, within 1 for text only, exactly **0 writes**). All three passed; the read figures of these tests are asserted ceilings, the write counts are exact.

Scenario `loadtest/profile_w3_part_a.js` (new, one file, `MODE=tabs|likes|suggestions`; header documents every knob), reads from the API request log with `node loadtest/analyze_w3_part_a.js api.log`. A fresh API process per run (cold caches), `CACHE_TTL` 60 s, `SUGGESTIONS_CACHE_TTL` 1 h.
API environment for every run: `ENV=local FEATURE_POSTS=on FEATURE_GRAPH=on FEATURE_ENGAGEMENT=on FEATURE_PROFILE_TABS=on FEATURE_LIKES_LIST=on FEATURE_FOLLOW_SUGGESTIONS=on RATE_LIMIT_PER_IP_PER_MIN=1000000 RATE_LIMIT_PRE_AUTH_IP_PER_MIN=1000000 RATE_LIMIT_USER_TIMELINE_PER_MIN=100000 RATE_LIMIT_LIKES_LIST_PER_MIN=100000 RATE_LIMIT_SUGGESTIONS_PER_MIN=100000 RATE_LIMIT_PROFILE_RELATIONSHIP_PER_MIN=100000`. Seeding only: `RATE_LIMIT_POST_CREATE_PER_MIN=100000 QUOTA_POSTS_PER_DAY=100000 QUOTA_NEW_ACCOUNT_POSTS_PER_DAY=100000 RATE_LIMIT_LIKES_PER_MIN=100000 QUOTA_LIKES_PER_DAY=100000 QUOTA_NEW_ACCOUNT_LIKES_PER_DAY=100000` (tabs, likes), plus `READ_BUDGET_PER_UID_PER_DAY=10000000` (likes, suggestions; **the tabs run kept the default 2000**) and `RATE_LIMIT_GRAPH_FOLLOW_PER_MIN=100000 QUOTA_FOLLOWS_PER_DAY=100000 QUOTA_NEW_ACCOUNT_FOLLOWS_PER_DAY=100000 GRAPH_MUTATIONS_PER_DAY=100000 SUGGESTED_USER_IDS=<12 uids from node loadtest/analyze_w3_part_a.js --curated-ids 12>` (suggestions). The Media tab selects on `hasMedia`, which only the image-upload path writes, so setup patches it through the Firestore emulator's REST API (no Vision path involved).

### Tabs, relationship (`MODE=tabs`: 1 author with 60 media posts, 30 plain posts, 60 reposts; 40 viewers)
Setup idles 65 s so every cache is cold. A solo VU runs first (viewer 0 on the Media tab, viewer 1 on `GetProfileRelationship` of another account), then 38 viewers do the profile-open flow, then a viral load of 15 calls/s for 20 s on the Media tab.
| Case | calls | `fs_reads` | server ms p50 / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Media p20, nothing cached anywhere (solo) | 1 | **24** | 18 |
| Media p20, second call | 1 | 20 | 6 |
| GetProfile of the author (the header; target cached by then) | 38 | 2 | 22 / 63 |
| GetProfileRelationship, nothing cached (solo) | 1 | **4** | 2 |
| GetProfileRelationship, repeat | 1 | **0** | 1 |
| GetProfileRelationship right after GetProfile | 38 | **1** | 27 / 58 |
| Media p20, first tab call after the header's calls | 38 (+ 1 later first call) | **21** (one `userLikes` read) | 59 / 88 |
| Media p20, later calls (second call of the flow, then the viral load) | about 340 | **20** | 7 / 12 (viral) |
| Reposts p20, first and second call | 38 + 38 | **20** and **20** | 58-61 / 90-99 |
| Media p50, first page (odd viewers) | 19 | **50** | 31 / 90 |
| Media p50, second page (the 10 rows left) | 19 | **10** | 18 / 32 |
- 0 writes, 0 ERROR lines, 0 failed requests, `read_budget_spent` rose by exactly `fs_reads` between consecutive calls in 531 of 531 pairs, and the largest `read_budget_spent` of any account in the run was 466 (the default limit is 2000). The k6 thresholds held: Media warm p95 88.8 ms, viral p95 14.8 ms (both under 400).
- **The viral profile is linear**: 300 calls on one author's Media tab read 6,000 documents in 20 seconds, 20 each, because the tabs have no result cache. At the per-viewer 30/min limit and 3 instances a single viewer cannot read more than 90 pages a minute (1,800 reads).
- The 54 at page 50 cold and the 24 / 54 ceilings are the chain budget tests below (`profile_tabs_integration_test.go`); the k6 run shows the warm and typical figures.

### Likes list (`MODE=likes PAIRS=2 LIKES=60 CAP_LIKES=1000`)
4 viewers with 60 likes of their own authors' posts (disjoint, so no viewer warms another's posts) plus one viewer at the 1,000 cap; setup idles 75 s; each pass lists every like once (k6 check green on all 9 passes).
| Case | calls | `fs_reads` | server ms p50 / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|
| page 20, cold, first page | 2 | **23** | 34 |
| page 20, cold, later pages (2 per viewer) | 4 | **20** | 7 / 8 |
| page 20, immediate second pass (all 20 posts of each page cached) | 6 | **0** | 1-3 |
| page 50, cold, first page | 2 (+ the cap viewer) | **53** | 37 / 40 |
| page 50, cold, second page (the 10 rows left) | 2 | **10** | 7 / 8 |
| page 50, immediate second pass | 4 | **0** | 2-3 |
| the cap viewer: 1,000 likes, 20 pages at 50 | 20 | **53** then 19 x **50** = **1,003** | 4-5 |
- 0 writes, 0 ERROR lines; the read budget was charged exactly (35 of 35 pairs). A scroll of the whole capped list is 1,003 reads, half of one instance's default 2000; at the default 10/min limit it takes two minutes.

### Suggestions (`MODE=suggestions`: 6 viewers each following 30 accounts, the 30 followees follow 4 of 60 candidates each, 12 curated uids, 3 viewers who follow nobody; then `FOLLOWEES=600 VIEWERS=1 CURATED_VIEWERS=0`)
Call 1 immediately after a 65 s settle, call 2 straight after, call 3 after 65 s (the 60 s profile cache expired, the 1 h suggestion entry not).
2026-10-28 note (ADR-0024 Amendment 2): this run is the record at `fcd0145` with `SUGGESTIONS_EDGE_LIMIT` 30, so a cold friends-of-friends call read 42. The default is now 20 (cap 32; applied in dev on 2026-10-28), so a rerun that does not set the variable should read 32 cold; set it to 30 to reproduce 42. A cold dev call at 32 or fewer is still owed.
| Case | calls | `fs_reads` | source / `candidates` | server ms p50 / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| friends of friends, cold (6 concurrent first calls) | 6 | **42** x 5, 33 x 1 | `fof` / 10 | 70 / 73 |
| friends of friends, call 2 (suggestion entry) | 6 | **0** | cache hit | 2 / 4 |
| friends of friends, call 3 (after the 60 s profile cache; 2 since L-1, see the build note) | 6 | **1** | cache hit | 19 / 23 |
| curated, cold (3 viewers, 12 ids) | 3 | **12** x 1, **2** x 2 (the curated profiles had just been cached by the first viewer) | `curated` / 10 | 20 / 23 |
| curated, call 2 | 3 | **0** | cache hit | 2 |
| **600 followees**: cold / call 2 / call 3 | 1 + 1 + 1 | **42** / 0 / 1 | `fof` / 10 | 193 / 1 / 10 |
- 0 writes, 0 ERROR lines, the read budget charged exactly (15 of 15 pairs, 2 of 2 at 600 followees). The cap does not grow with the number followed (30 and 600 followees both read 42). All six first calls of the run were concurrent, which is why only one of them saw profiles cached by the others (33); with few concurrent users at Stage 0 the typical cold call is 42.

### Budget tests re-run on the same emulators (`go test -tags=integration -v`, the repository's `budgettest` lines; output kept with the run)
| Test | Measured |
